# System Risk Register

Status: Active

> Closed entries live in [`archive/system_risk_register_resolved_20260726.md`](archive/system_risk_register_resolved_20260726.md). This file is the live open-risk queue only;
> an archived entry keeps its `R-` id, so a SESSION_LOG pointer to a closed finding still resolves there.

### R-ASHORT-THEME-FINGERPRINT-CONVERGENCE-BEFORE-FREEZE - two fingerprint cleanups that are free now and cost the whole clock later

- **Status \ severity**: **open P3, pre-freeze only** (A-short theme forward comparison contract binding; raised 2026-07-26 by Claude Code in the round-3 review of `35fd112f`, promoted out of that entry so it survives its closure). Comparison-only, nothing misreports today — while the track is `pre_freeze_audit_only` the fingerprint is a stable constant, so neither item can bite. They become expensive the moment `--start-epoch` runs.
- **Item 1 — dead helper is still fingerprint-bound**: `engine/a_short_theme_forward_comparison.py::_semantic_file_function_digest` has zero callers tree-wide, but `_contract_function_semantics()` digests every module function, so it is one of the 71 bound functions (reviewer-measured). Deleting it therefore moves `contract_fingerprint`.
- **Item 2 — the engine's own constants are bound by a hand-maintained inclusion list, the opposite polarity to every other file**: `_semantic_file_contract_digest` auto-binds any UPPER-case constant referenced by the bound closure **by value** for `runners/a_short_theme_forward_comparison.py`, `runners/backtest_rank.py` and `runners/forward_tracker.py`; but the engine module's own constants are bound only if a human remembered to list them inside `comparison_contract_fingerprint`. Reviewer probe on `35fd112f`: changing `ADMISSION_GRACE_CALENDAR_DAYS` 3→30 moves the fingerprint (**True**), changing `RETURN_WINDOW_DAYS` 10→20 moves it (**True**), but replacing `ADMISSION_TIME_PROVENANCE` wholesale does **not** (**False**) because it is referenced inside a bound function yet absent from the list — a function's AST records the constant's *name*, never its *value*. No gap is live today because every behaviour-bearing constant happens to be listed; the risk is the next one that is not.
- **Required repair (one slice, before any `--start-epoch`)**: delete `_semantic_file_function_digest`; add module-constant auto-binding for the engine itself (snapshot every module-level UPPER-case constant's value into the digest, keeping the explicit list only as belt-and-braces); add a reverse control asserting that changing *any* module constant's value moves `contract_fingerprint`, so the probe above becomes a machine assertion instead of a reviewer note.
- **Why the ordering is the whole point**: pre-freeze, `fingerprint_or_pre_freeze` returns a constant, so both edits are free. Post-freeze, either edit changes the real digest, `_epoch_context` returns `epoch_contract_mismatch`, `epoch_clock_weeks` drops to 0 and every criterion becomes `evidence_blocked` — i.e. doing this cleanup after the clock starts forfeits every accumulated week and forces an epoch reset from zero.
- **Boundary**: comparison-only, no EGS selection/ranking/veto/sizing, M6.7 output, provider, account or order behaviour.

### R-ASHORT-EPOCH-CHURN-TEST-MUTATES-PRODUCTION-SOURCES-IN-PLACE - a lane test appends to three real production files and can leave the residue behind

- **Status \ severity**: **open P2** (A-short evidence-epoch test hygiene; found 2026-07-26 by Claude Code while running the merge-moment full lane pack for the theme-adjudicator slice). Test-side — no engine computes a wrong answer, no EGS selection/ranking/veto/sizing, M6.7 output, provider, account or order behaviour. P2 not P3 because the failure mode writes into production source files and those bytes are epoch-fingerprint inputs.
- **Finding (observed, not hypothetical)**: `tests/test_a_short_evidence_epoch_mode.py::test_unrelated_source_edits_do_not_move_any_epoch:191-201` appends `\n# evidence-epoch churn probe\n` to three real tracked sources — `A-EGS/egs_main.py`, `runners/a_short_phase5_engine.py`, `runners/a_short_weekly_pipeline.py` — and restores them in a bare `finally` loop via `write_bytes`. During the full pack the **restore itself** raised `OSError: [Errno 22] Invalid argument` on `runners/a_short_weekly_pipeline.py`; since the loop is unguarded, the exception aborted it before the remaining files were rewritten, and all three were left carrying the probe comment in the working tree. The reviewer found it only by running `git status` after the pack, and removed the six residual lines by exact edit (`git checkout --` was refused by the local permission classifier).
- **Why it matters beyond one red test**: those three files' bytes are inputs to the post-freeze epoch fingerprints of the other six comparison tracks, so residue silently moves contracts that are supposed to be frozen; and an executor or reviewer who runs the pack and then `git add -A` commits a stray comment into a production entrypoint. Nothing in the pack detects the residue — the test that caused it is the same one that failed, so its own end-state assertion never ran.
- **Reproduction status**: **intermittent**. An immediate identical re-run on the same code state (same 1997-test pack) passed this test and left the tree byte-clean, so the OSError is transient (Windows write contention, not a logic defect). Intermittency lowers the frequency, not the blast radius.
- **Required repair**: stop mutating tracked production sources in place — copy them into a temp directory and point the fingerprint path constants there for the duration of the probe. If in-place mutation is deliberately kept, then the restore must be per-file `try/except` so one failure cannot skip the others, must re-read and verify the restored bytes, and must fail loudly naming any file left modified. Add a reverse control that simulates a failing restore and asserts the test reports dirty files rather than exiting silently.
- **Boundary**: test-side only. No provider, live weekly, account, order, broker, production switch, EGS selection or M6.7 byte change. Not caused by and not blocking for the theme-adjudicator slice; the slice's own full pack is otherwise green against the known master baseline.

### R-ASHORT-PUBLIC-COMPARISON-SUMMARY-NO-ASOF-MONOTONICITY - a stale `--as-of` run silently overwrites a newer published comparison summary

- **Status / severity**: **open P2** (A-short comparison-only public artifacts; found 2026-07-25 by Claude Code while disposing of the only dirt in the working tree). Comparison-only — no EGS selection/ranking/veto/sizing, M6.7 output, provider, account or order behaviour. P2 not P1 because nothing misreports today: `p3b_ready` is unreachable (`public_verdict` is hard-coded `not_adjudicated`) and every track is pre-freeze, so no clock can advance off the corrupted number. It becomes live the moment the clocks start.
- **Finding (class, not instance)**: `runners/a_short_target_policy_comparison_runner.py::write_public_summary:478-485` validates schema / message / review_state / admission binding and then `_atomic_write`s **unconditionally**. `as_of` is caller-supplied (`args.as_of`, line 771) and nothing compares it against the `as_of` already present in the tracked file, so `--as-of <older>` silently republishes an older, emptier summary over a newer one. Sibling sweep: `engine/a_short_overlay_adjudication.py::write_public_summary:886-887` has the identical shape (`validate_public_summary(summary); _write(json_path, summary)`) and the same gap — 2 of the tracked public comparison summaries confirmed, so this is a class.
- **Observed instance (the recurring worktree dirt)**: committed `research/results/a_short/target_policy_comparison_summary.{json,md}` = `as_of 20260727 / data_through 20260727 / forward_weeks 1` (committed in `46dd7488`, the 20260727 weekly evidence commit). Working tree = `as_of 20260609 / data_through null / forward_weeks 0`, i.e. a run driven with `--as-of 20260609`. Mixed truth: `forward_weeks 0` is the **honest** post-pre-freeze value (the constant epoch of `7d72679d` legitimately leaves the one previously captured week outside the current epoch — the documented one-time switchover cost), while `as_of 20260609` is **wrong** — a published summary's clock went backwards.
- **Why the previous disposition failed**: this was noted twice inside other entries' Boundary/Scope lines only (`R-ASHORT-OVERLAY-EVAL-RETIREMENT-README-CURRENT-FACT-LOSS` scope manifest: "the P2 public-summary regression is a separate item"; and a Boundary line recording it was "reverted to HEAD, not swept in"). It never got its own entry, Required, or closure condition, and revert-and-forget treats the symptom: the file is dirty again. The 20260727 published bundle embeds its own point-in-time copy (`as_of 20260727 / data_through null / forward_weeks 0`) and is not rewritten by this, so no published bundle is corrupted.
- **Required repair (class-level, one fix for both writers)**: before writing a tracked public comparison summary, read the existing tracked file and refuse to overwrite it with a strictly older `as_of` (fail loudly, do not silently no-op); a same-`as_of` rewrite stays allowed so a legitimate re-settle can refresh counts. Apply to `a_short_target_policy_comparison_runner.write_public_summary` and `a_short_overlay_adjudication.write_public_summary` in the same slice, plus a reverse-control test per writer (older `as_of` raises; equal/newer `as_of` writes). Then decide the artifact's correct current content in that same slice — regenerate at the canonical `as_of` rather than hand-editing either number.
- **Do not**: do not commit the current worktree state (it would publish a backwards clock), and do not simply `git checkout --` it again without the guard — that is the disposition that already failed twice.
- **Boundary**: comparison-only public artifacts. No provider, live weekly, account, order, broker, production switch, EGS selection or M6.7 byte change. Not a P4a behaviour change: P4a's adjudication logic and epoch are untouched by this Required; only its summary-write path gains the same guard.

### R-ASHORT-MASTER-LANE-PACK-TWO-PREEXISTING-RED - the A-short lane pack is already red on master, so "the pack is green" cannot be used as a regression baseline

- **Status / severity**: **open P2** (A-short lane test integrity; found 2026-07-26 by Claude Code while attributing the knife-1 full-pack failures). Test-side only — no EGS selection/ranking/veto/sizing, M6.7 output, provider, account or order behaviour. P2 because nothing computes a wrong answer, but a permanently red baseline hides the next real regression and forces every reviewer to re-derive attribution by hand, which is exactly the cost this round paid.
- **Finding (measured on master `8a9d5d83`, pinned main Python)**: two targets fail on master itself. (a) `tests.test_a_short_semantic_risk_contract_docs.SemanticRiskContractDocs.test_current_official_advisory_requires_human_confirmation` asserts `"event-bound human decision"` appears in `docs/README.md`; `grep -c` on master's `docs/README.md` returns 0, and the test file is byte-identical between master and the Codex worktree, so the README lost (or never gained) the phrase the guard pins. The phrase is also absent at `7d72679d`, so this predates the overlay-eval retirement rather than being caused by it. (b) `tests.test_a_short_review1_knives_6_10.RunIdentityAndPublishTest.test_weekly_consumer_rejects_analysis_bytes_not_bound_by_marker` errors with `json.decoder.JSONDecodeError: Expecting value: line 1 column 1 (char 0)` from `runners/a_short_weekly_pipeline.py:172` `_validate_official_publish_marker`, i.e. the analysis path it is handed is empty or non-JSON.
- **Required repair**: decide per target whether the guard or the artifact is wrong — for (a) either restore the pinned phrase to `docs/README.md` or retire the assertion if the contract wording legitimately moved (do not weaken it silently); for (b) fix the fixture/setup so the consumer receives real JSON, since an error is not the negative control the test name promises (it should reject on the marker binding, not die on a parse). Re-run both on master and record the green.
- **Boundary**: test/doc-side only. No provider, live weekly, account, order, broker, production switch, EGS selection or M6.7 byte change. Not caused by, and not blocking for, the theme-field knife-1 slice; it is recorded here because that slice's review had to establish it.

### R-ASHORT-COMPARISON-EPOCH-CHURN-WITHOUT-PROTECTION - epoch fingerprints hashed moving implementation files, silently voiding every accumulated week

- **Status / severity**: **repair landed in `7d72679d`; entry stays OPEN P1 until the switchover** (all six A-short comparison tracks: P0/v2, P1, P2, P3, P4a, P5; user-directed 2026-07-25 after review surfaced the mechanism firing twice in one week). The pre-freeze mode, the four verdict gates and the churn guard are all committed and reviewed; what keeps this open is the Restore condition below — flipping `MODE` to `frozen_enforced` without first converging the fingerprints onto semantic contracts would bring the original churn back at exactly the moment it starts costing real forward evidence. Comparison-only — no EGS selection/ranking/veto/sizing, M6.7 output, provider, account or order behavior changes. Severity is P1 because the defect made every forward-evidence clock unable to advance, which is the whole point of those tracks.
- **Finding (class, not instance)**: each track's epoch fingerprint hashed WHOLE implementation files — among them `runners/a_short_weekly_pipeline.py`, `runners/a_short_phase5_engine.py`, `A-EGS/egs_main.py`, weekly schemas and runtime presets. Any edit unrelated to a comparison contract therefore moved the fingerprint and silently excluded every accumulated week (`_current_records()` -> 0, or the active epoch simply not matching), with no warning. Measured twice in one week: commit `f31b3971` orphaned P4a (current `178925…` vs ledger `576d2d…`); the overlay-retirement slice changed 2 of the 10 files in P3's list and orphaned P3 (`a9b22f17…` vs `62c5808a…`). Meanwhile the tracks hold 0-1 weeks each against 12/24/36-week checkpoints, so the mechanism protected almost nothing while invalidating everything. **Enumeration correction**: the initial desktop analysis named only 4 tracks; the real class is 6 — `engine/a_short_regime_action_comparison.py::_runtime_policy_source_fingerprint` (P1, 13 whole files) and `engine/a_short_factor_comparison_v2.py::_canonical_contracts` (P0/v2, `runtime_wiring_contract` + two `phase5_source` legs) have the same disease and were missed.
- **Repair (2026-07-25)**: added `engine/a_short_evidence_epoch_mode.py` as the single authority. While `MODE = "pre_freeze_audit_only"` every track's fingerprint is a stable per-track constant (sha256-shaped so existing `^[0-9a-f]{64}$` schemas still validate), captures still record a fingerprint, and nothing is dropped by unrelated edits. The real computations were NOT deleted — each moved to a `_real_*` function and returns as soon as `MODE` is flipped to `frozen_enforced`. Because a pre-freeze constant can never equal a real fingerprint, flipping the mode naturally leaves pre-freeze weeks outside the new epoch, so the 12/24/36-week clocks start at the freeze. Pre-freeze evidence is audit-only: `evidence_counts_toward_clock()` is False and gates P1 `ready`, P4a `_adjudicate`, P5 `p5b_build_due`/notice and P2 `_current_review_status` to their neutral outcomes, so no track can conclude on evidence that does not count.
- **One-time switchover cost (verified benign)**: the four tracks holding records (P4a/P3 already orphaned, P2 two tracks and P5 previously intact, 1 week each) fall outside the constant epoch at switchover WITHOUT raising — `_records`/`_capture_records` still load and validate them, only the current-epoch filters exclude them. They are re-captured on the next weekly run and stable from then on. At most one audit-only week per track was affected.
- **Closure evidence**: `tests/test_a_short_evidence_epoch_mode.py` (9 new guards) — appending a comment to `weekly_pipeline.py` + `phase5_engine.py` + `egs_main.py` leaves all 10 epoch components (6 tracks; P2 has 2 components and P0/v2 has 4 contract legs) unchanged; flipping to `frozen_enforced` restores real, drift-sensitive fingerprints for every one of them (proves parked-not-deleted); unknown mode and unregistered track fail closed; the four verdict gates return neutral. Six-track pack (v2 ×4, final-action, industry-weight, overlay-adjudication, regime-action, regime-comparison ×2, target-policy) plus the new module = 226 OK under the pinned main Python. `py_compile` clean; `git diff --check` shows CRLF warnings only. The 13 pre-existing cases that assert enforced-mode drift were pointed at `frozen_enforced` via `setUp`, so the enforced contract keeps full coverage instead of being weakened.
- **Restore condition**: flip `MODE` to `frozen_enforced` once the design is settled. **Before flipping**, converge each track's real fingerprint from whole-file bytes onto semantic contracts (governance/preset/schema/admission + `inspect.getsource` of evidence-producing functions) and make retained file hashes LF-canonical. **This convergence is a hard prerequisite, not advice, and currently has no machine guard (Claude Code 2026-07-25).** The capture side already restarts cleanly by itself — every track's `_active_epoch(..., create=True)` auto-creates an empty epoch the first time it sees the new fingerprint, so recording resumes with a zeroed clock on the first weekly run after the flip with no manual ledger work. The danger is the week AFTER that: `tests/test_a_short_evidence_epoch_mode.py::test_enforcement_is_parked_not_deleted` asserts the enforced fingerprints differ from the parked constants, i.e. post-flip they are once again whole-file-sensitive, so the first unrelated edit to `runners/a_short_weekly_pipeline.py` / `runners/a_short_phase5_engine.py` / `A-EGS/egs_main.py` re-orphans every accumulated week — the original defect, deferred to the moment it starts costing real forward evidence. **Closure requirement for the flip slice**: land the semantic convergence in the SAME slice as the `MODE` flip; do not flip without it. `test_unrelated_source_edits_do_not_move_any_epoch` already measures the enforced side — it asserts the churn edit moves exactly `CHURN_BOUND_COMPONENTS` = {`p0v2.decision_delta_contract`, `p0v2.immutable_common_pool_contract`, `p1`, `p3`, `p4a`}. Convergence is complete when that set is empty, and the test fails until the constant is updated to match, so the flip cannot quietly skip it. Note the adjudicator binding must stay until P4a's hardcoded 12/24/36 · 6/12/18 · 0.25 · 0.55 thresholds move into governance/registry, otherwise unbinding it would allow a post-hoc threshold change with no epoch reset; P0/v2 documents its adjudication binding as deliberate.
- **Pre-freeze fifth-knife disposition (2026-07-25)**: `engine/a_short_overlay_adjudication.py` is pinned as `p4a_overlay_epoch` and has drifted (`2b495f20…` -> `628a443d…`, LF-canonical) because this repair adds the conservative pre-freeze no-adjudication gate; `schemas/a_short_m67_effect_contract.json` has also drifted (`330bb406…` -> `6f91f515…`) through hash-only metadata changes. These are expected information while the packet remains `frozen_not_started`, has zero observed forward-live months, and `evidence_epoch_mode.enforcement_enabled()` is false. The freeze test verifies those honesty fields in pre-freeze and defers hash equality until enforcement is enabled; it does not erase the hashes or claim the drift is harmless. At switchover, rehash all eight contracts LF-canonically, recompute P4a's semantic fingerprint and packet self-hash, then record separate explicit epoch judgments: effect-contract may remain same epoch as hash-only; P4a is behavioral but conservative with zero countable evidence, so may remain same epoch only with that recorded judgment. The enforced-mode guard mutation must reject each drift.
- **Boundary**: comparison-only; no provider, live weekly, account, order, broker, production switch, or selection behavior change. A new comparison track MUST register in `TRACKS` and route its fingerprint through this module.

### R-ASHORT-FIFTH-KNIFE-FORWARD-EVIDENCE-EPOCH - repaired code is not proof of strategy effectiveness

- **Status / severity**: **in_progress P1** (A-short forward evidence and ship-gate sufficiency; 2026-07-24 freeze-packet step reviewed PASS by Claude Code; risk stays OPEN pending ≥12-month live forward evidence + ship-gate adjudication — code repair is not evidence closure).
- **Fifth-knife freeze reconciled (2026-07-24, Claude Code)**: the P1/P2 fixes changed two frozen contracts (`schemas/a_short_m67_effect_contract.json`, `schemas/a_short_regime_action_comparison_weekly.schema.json`). Because `observed_forward_live_months=0` (epoch not started, zero forward evidence) and both were same-day bug-fixes (the frozen action schema was itself the one that crashed the V14.3 runner every week), the packet was resealed to the corrected contracts — same epoch, NOT a new split: all 8 `frozen_contracts` rehashed to an LF-canonical basis (checkout-independent), `source_revision.git_commit` corrected `f163dea0`→`a40c2fc1` (the fourth-knife PASS merge, which includes the effect-contract reseal that `f163dea0` predated), and `record_sha256` re-derived. Also fixed a governance gap found during P2 review: nothing machine-guarded the frozen-contract shas (the freeze test only checked the packet's own `record_sha256` self-hash), so a frozen-contract change was silently undetectable (the freeze test stayed green while a contract drifted). Added `tests/schema/test_a_short_fifth_knife_forward_evidence_freeze_schema.py::test_frozen_contracts_recompute_match_their_files`, which recomputes every frozen contract LF-canonically and FAILS on drift, forcing a future reseal / new-epoch decision. Freeze test 3 OK. The ship-gate/evidence risk stays OPEN (still needs ≥12-month live forward evidence; the reseal does not advance evidence).
- **Finding**: The first four knives established correct execution, date/PIT identity, lifecycle disposition, and P4 terminal reporting. They do not establish strategy effectiveness. Existing historical evidence remains `risk_filter_only` and cannot be relabeled as live forward evidence.
- **Required repair**: Freeze one new evidence epoch after the fourth-knife PASS; bind commit, runtime policies, V14.3/P4 comparison contracts, schemas, three clocks, canonical weekly cohort, costs/slippage, cash drag, unfilled, exits, benchmarks, and regime/month/industry slices. Historical replay is audit-only; any rule change opens a new epoch; no automatic order or production switch is allowed.
- **Codex repair (2026-07-24)**: Added the schema-validated and self-hashed freeze packet `docs/a_short_fifth_knife_forward_evidence_freeze_20260724.json`, with the current fourth-knife source revision and live-only capture boundary. The packet records `observed_forward_live_months=0`, `status=not_evaluable_forward_evidence_insufficient`, and `risk_filter_only`; it intentionally does not run provider/live capture or claim ship-gate progress.
- **Closure evidence for this step**: Pinned-main-Python freeze/schema + V14.3/P4/effect-contract/route-doc pack = 79 OK; PowerShell parse and diff check passed. No provider, live weekly, account, order, broker, or automatic switch ran.
- **Completion boundary**: This risk stays open until the required forward evidence is actually accumulated and independently adjudicated. Only all ship gates — monthly alpha t-stat >=2.0, Sharpe >=1.0, max drawdown <=15%, and >=12 months forward live evidence — may change the disposition from `risk_filter_only`.
- **Independent review PASS (2026-07-24, Claude Code) — freeze-packet step only, not effectiveness**: verified the epoch-freeze artifact itself. Baseline correct: `source_revision.git_commit` == current HEAD `f163dea08986…` (the reviewed fourth-knife commit). Integrity (pinned main Python probe): all 8 `frozen_contracts` sha256 recompute-match the real files (screening + m67 runtime policies, V14.3 governance + schemas, P4 overlay engine, m67 effect-contract, weekly-report schema); `record_sha256` self-hash recomputes correctly. Honesty is MACHINE-enforced — the schema pins `effectiveness_claimed:false`, `current_disposition:risk_filter_only`, `ship_gate.status:not_evaluable…`, `observed_forward_live_months:0`, `production_promotion_allowed:false`, `automatic_orders_allowed:false`, `status:frozen_not_started`, `historical_replay_counts_as_forward:false` as consts with `additionalProperties:false`, so the packet cannot schema-validate while claiming efficacy/promotion/started. No secret/account/ticker/price leak (governance metadata only). Conforms to §第五刀 (records commit + policies + contracts + schemas; new epoch; prior audit-only; one canonical cohort/week; costs/slippage/cash-drag/unfilled/exits/benchmarks/regime-month-industry capture fields; no post-hoc threshold changes). `tests.schema.test_a_short_fifth_knife_forward_evidence_freeze_schema` = 2 OK. No independent agent / no full lane (schema-first no-code artifact). This closes ONLY the freeze step; the risk stays open until real ≥12-month forward evidence is accumulated and adjudicated.

### R-ASHORT-THIRD-KNIFE-THREE-CLOCK-CONTRACT - decision, runtime, and settled-price clocks could drift

- **Status / severity**: **in_progress P1** (A-short PIT/date identity and forward-evidence integrity; 2026-07-24 Codex repair → Claude Code independent review PASS; commit deferred to batch close pending the second-knife follow-up re-review; three LOW defense-in-depth Optionals below).
- **Finding**: The A-short path previously let Monday decision artifacts request Monday prices, used decision date for industry expectations, and required V14.3 forward eligibility to use same-day equality. P4 also encoded `decision_date` as its future-price request date. This could accept a future EOD bar, mislabel a Friday price as Monday, or make a valid Sunday→Monday live run ineligible.
- **Required repair**: Carry `decision_as_of`, `run_date`, and official settled `price_data_through` as separate clocks. EGS now accepts `--price-as-of` and binds official calendar/cache/data requests to it; canonical weekly routing passes resolver `last_settled`; weekly artifacts, source identity, receipts, candidate quote bounds, and industry signal dates bind to the price clock; V14.3 uses live/receipt/latest-settled gates; P4 derives its request date from accepted M6.7 price freshness. Missing, future, or mismatched clocks fail closed.
- **Closure evidence**: Sunday→Monday date-matrix, official-date/cache-key, industry, forward-eligibility, P4 future-price, receipt-tamper, schema, effect-contract, and route-doc regressions passed under the pinned main Python. Combined third-knife/Phase5/M6.7/V14.3/P4 pack: 426 OK; schema/effect/route gates: 69 OK; weekly main-chain/effect-contract subset: 77 OK; changed Python files compile. No provider, live weekly, account, order, or broker execution ran.
- **Independent review PASS (2026-07-24, Claude Code)**: full read of `A-EGS/egs_main.py` / `a_short_weekly_pipeline.py` / `weekly_screening.ps1` / V14.3 `a_short_regime_action_comparison.py`+`a_short_regime_comparison_runner.py` / `analysis_input_contract.py` / P4 `a_short_overlay_adjudication.py` / effect-contract, plus one worktree-scoped adversarial agent that cleared all five angles (look-ahead / forward-forgery / fail-closed / industry / P4) with NO high finding. #1 fixed (launcher passes resolver `last_settled` as `--price-as-of`; `get_trade_dates`/`get_daily_all`/qfq cache key all rebind to the settled day, never the unsettled decision day). #5 fixed (industry `expected_as_of`+`source_as_of`+candidate-quote PIT rebind to `price_data_through`; real mismatch → `unknown`). #6 fixed + forgery-resistant (`action_run_date=_current_run_date()` real day → a historical replay is `historical_replay` → `forward_eligible=False`; `validate_action_record`+`refresh_action_records` re-derive and reject drift; ±7-day decision/settled bound; canonical Sunday→Monday eligible). #7/#8 fixed (P4 `price_request.price_data_through` derives from accepted M6.7 `run_lineage.price_freshness`, absent → raise). PIT is TIGHTENED to `price_data_through` (a Monday quote is now rejected as look-ahead). The effect-contract change is strengthening (adds the three clocks + `source.clocks` as `must_affect_result`). Reviewer focused pack `tests.test_a_short_third_knife_clocks tests.test_a_short_regime_action_comparison tests.test_a_short_overlay_adjudication tests.test_a_short_industry_theme` = 61 OK under the pinned main Python; full lane not run (per user). Commit deferred: the second-knife follow-up repair is pending independent re-review and its phase5 change is intermingled in this worktree, so the batch commits after that re-review passes.
- **Optional (non-blocking, third knife — all LOW / defense-in-depth)**: (F1) `engine/data/analysis_input_contract.py:96-108` only enforces `price_data_through <= trade_date` inside `if clocks:`; a hand-crafted artifact omitting `source.clocks` with a future top-level `price_data_through` (and that future date seeded into `recent_trade_dates`) accepts a future quote — unreachable via the real producer (egs always emits `source.clocks`+top-level and `run_egs` guards `price_data_through > TODAY`) and the weekly pipeline re-aborts on `price_data_through > as_of`; one-line hardening = hoist an unconditional `if price_data_through > trade_date: raise` out of the `if clocks:` block. (F2) `runners/a_short_regime_comparison_runner.py:795` sets `price_day_latest_settled = price <= decision` (essentially always true), so 'price == latest settled' is not actually enforced — NOT a forgery/look-ahead vector (forward returns are measured from `decision`; the real gates = live `capture_mode` + `source_receipt_complete` are unaffected), and the canonical launcher passes the genuine `last_settled`. (F3-cosmetic) two `decision_predicate_sha256` lines in `schemas/a_short_m67_effect_contract.json` sit at 6-space vs 4-space indent (JSON-valid). None block; can fold into the second-knife follow-up.

### R-USSHORT-RUNTEST-AUTO-BUDGET-AUTH-AND-ACCOUNT-FACT-REWRITE - auto-budget entrypoint bypasses the live authorization boundary and relabels stale account facts

- **Status / severity**: **in_progress P1** (US-short provider-execution authorization and account/PIT integrity; 2026-07-24 Codex repair awaits independent review). No provider call was made during review or repair.
- **Finding**: The repair changes `run_weekly_capstone()` so every `dry_run=False` call unconditionally replaces `confirm_user_authorization` with `True`. This bypasses the existing direct/API fail-closed boundary; a caller can select the injected/live path without the former explicit authorization fact, while the original `test_live_run_requires_authorization` still passes only because a later missing-budget error is also a `WeeklyCapstoneError`. Separately, `_prepare_account_state_input()` calls `roll_forward_account_state()` on an older JSON snapshot and rewrites its top-level `as_of` plus reconciliation/evidence dates to the decision date. Its sidecar calls the source facts stale, but the consumed account-state artifact itself now claims `as_of=decision_date`; this is exactly the forbidden `20260720` -> `20260723` relabelling, not a fresh paper-account preparation from the source tables.
- **Required repair**: Keep the one-click `-Live` auto-derived immutable Pass2 budget, but retain a non-forgeable explicit live authorization boundary for direct/API execution and prove `confirm_user_authorization=False` fails before account preparation, transactions, or any stage/provider seam. The PowerShell entrypoint may supply that fact for an explicit `-Live` command; it must not make every Python `dry_run=False` call authorized. Remove JSON account-state date rewriting. A target-date account state must be generated from the private manual input tables through the existing converter/lineage path (or the run must fail closed); retain the original facts date/staleness honestly and add a regression that an older JSON cannot be relabelled into a target-date execution input.
- **Closure tests**: pinned-main-Python tests must distinguish the authorization error from later validation errors, assert no injected stage/account-preparation write occurs without authorization, preserve positive `-Live` wrapper wiring with auto-derived/frozen approval and existing pace/429/physical-attempt caps, and reject an older JSON-only snapshot while accepting a genuinely target-date converter output with correct lineage.
- **Codex repair (2026-07-24; pending independent review)**: removed the JSON account-state roll-forward/relabel helper and its sibling-file auto-selection. The capstone again rejects an unconfirmed live API call before pipeline execution; the PowerShell `-Live` wrapper explicitly supplies `--confirm-user-authorization` together with `--auto-pass2-budget`, so one user command remains sufficient and no user-typed budget/cost cap is restored. The converter remains the sole target-date preparation path. Focused pinned-main-Python capstone/account/runtest pack: 172 OK; no provider or full runtest execution.

## Enforcement Rules

- Every material review or audit finding that affects data integrity, PIT safety, schema contract, execution simulation, security, ship-gate evidence, or cross-LLM continuity must be either fixed in the same reviewed slice or entered here before the round ends.
- `执行` must check this register before choosing the next task. Open P0 items outrank normal roadmap work unless the user explicitly approves a narrower override.
- `审查` must verify that new material findings were either fixed or registered here, and that resolved entries include concrete verification.
- `修复` may update this register only for approved Required fixes or Optional dispositions being handled in that repair round.
- A risk entry is not closed by intent. Closure requires file / test evidence and a reviewed commit or an explicit accepted-risk decision from the user.

## Status And Severity

Severity:
- P0: blocks the next unsafe execution path or can contaminate evidence / official outputs.
- P1: high risk before broader implementation, provider work, long-lane work, or production-like use.
- P2: medium risk; must be queued and fixed before the affected subsystem is promoted.
- P3: low risk or documentation hygiene.

Status:
- `open`: accepted for tracking; not fixed.
- `in_progress`: current reviewed change is addressing it.
- `blocked`: needs user decision or external dependency.
- `resolved`: fixed and verified.
- `superseded`: replaced by more specific open / resolved entries; the underlying risk is not fixed merely because the parent summary was split.
- `accepted_risk`: user explicitly accepted the residual risk.
- `needs_revalidation`: audit claim is plausible enough to track, but line-level repo validation is still required before implementation.

- **Design / invariants (§12.3)**: `run_account_mode ∈ {paper_only, manual_actual, dual}`（paper state 永不覆盖真实账户）；时序铁律 = 先成熟旧周（只用 ≤ price_basis 的 bar）再冻结新周（禁 look-ahead / backfill）；同 decision_date 幂等 + 盘前只 supersede pending + 成熟后 immutable；`NAV = cash + Σ(remaining×mark)` 且已实现盈亏不重复加；持仓动作成交合同（建/减[卖 recommended_action_shares]/清仓-止损·止盈[gap-aware]/事件[source-bound 齐才成交]/加仓 fail-closed）；诊断 NAV 可出但复权未确认 → `paper_evaluable=false`；独立 `us_short_model_paper_portfolio_state` schema + $100k→$300k adapter（仅满足 ÷3、非真实资产）；私密 `state/us_short/model_paper_private/` 原子事务、无半状态。
- **Cut plan (Codex 执行 + Claude 复审)**: 刀1 合同 + 纯状态迁移引擎（schema 冻结 + 成熟/fills/mark 纯函数 + 持仓动作成交表 + 加仓 fail-closed；不持久化/不接 capstone/不 provider）→ 刀2 私密事务 store（原子 head/digest 链/幂等/supersede/崩溃恢复）→ 刀3 offline capstone 双账户分支（fixture 下 paper_only + dual；stage 序 = 成熟旧周→prior guard→current decision→冻结新 bundle；manual∪paper 去重预算但不 fetch）→ 刀4 gated weekly 集成 + 首真实周（预算走 §18.1 #32 auto-derive/no-cap：用户不授权 budget 数字、一键命令本身=该次 provider 授权；无未来 bar 不伪造成交）→ 刀5（后置）可比 NAV 接口（不改 comparison-only 预注册）。每刀 US-short-only / schema-first / offline fixture 可测 / 失败无半状态 / never ship-gate。桌面完整设计 = `C:\Users\cnhea\Desktop\us_short主系统模拟盘方案.md`（评估后修订版）。
- **Boundary**: 不接券商 / 不下单 / 不读真实券商账户 / 不自动换股·记现金·强平；不碰 A 股 / US-long；paper 永不判 full-size ship-gate；真实 provider 周运行的授权 = 用户每周运行一键命令本身（无 budget 数字，见 `R-USSHORT-CAPSTONE-STANDING-PASS2-BUDGET-CAP-ONECLICK`）。
- **刀3/刀4 implementation (2026-07-22)**: `default_pipeline()` 现含 `model_paper_adapter`（OHLCV 到达后只在内存成熟旧周、派生 adapter 并写入既有 account seam）与 `model_paper_weekly`（`weekly_bridge` emitted 后才消费同周 machine record，原子结算/冻结并在私密周报写七项组合字段）。NO-EMIT 不进 terminal stage；首周只由 terminal transition 播种 $100k，后续 launcher 保留 adapter slot、store 只从 head 续仓。`us_short_paper_one_click.py` 将命令调用传为 auto-derived Pass2 budget + `model_paper_store_root`；无额外内部 cap、无用户预算数字。离线 5 周 fixture 断言 week5 `cumulative_pnl = current_nav - 100000`、连续周数=5、仅首周 seed；未跑 provider/首真实周。

### R-USSHORT-CAPSTONE-STANDING-PASS2-BUDGET-CAP-ONECLICK - operator wants a true one-click weekly run; the current flow needs a per-week budget preview + separate re-authorization each time

- **Status / severity**: **open — user-approved direction (2026-07-20; refined 2026-07-22: auto-derive, no user-typed number); design in `docs/us_short_system_design.md` §18.1 #32; slice pending Codex** (P3 operability; US-short only). §18.0 P0 provider-authorization gate 的**兼容细化、不放宽**；authorizes no new provider boundary by itself.
- **Finding / context**: `R-USSHORT-CAPSTONE-PASS2-BUDGET-PREVIEW-CIRCULAR-GATE`（resolved）曾给出 `--prepare-pass2-budget` 预览 → 逐次授权 → `--pass2-call-budget N` 的流程（每周 preview ~2h + 单独重授权，非真·一键）。用户（2026-07-20）要"固定上限一键"；**2026-07-22 进一步明确：连"设上限"这步也不要——因为设上限本身仍要用户去算/知道该填多少**。故方向细化为"自动预算一键、用户不填任何数字"。（未提交的 `runners/us_short_paper_one_click.py` 已 `auto_authorize_pass2_budget=True`+`authorized_pass2_call_budget=None`，即已部分实现自动派生；缺的是写进权威 spec + 内部安全天花板。）
- **Design / invariant (§18.1 #32, 2026-07-22 final — no cap)**: 一键 paper 周跑**不要求用户预先计算/填写任何预算数字，也不设任何配额上限/内部天花板**；系统同一次 run 内从 preflight 漏斗**自动派生** Pass2 call 数并直接用之（只记录供审计、非需预配置的门）。**结构性边界 = `momentum_top_k`**（选股参数、非预算；默认 200、硬上限 250、代码强制）+ 顶 K 漏斗：FMP grades = 每 Pass2 目标 1 次调用、目标数 ≤ `top_k ∪ 持仓` ≤ ~250 → 默认 K 下 grades 日量结构上 ≤ 免费档日上限，**无需单独 cap**；Massive 走既有 pacing + retry-on-429，SEC 公共源。**一键命令本身 = 该次 provider 授权动作**（§18.0"预算须显式批准"由"命令调用=授权 + `top_k` 硬上限 250=代码强制的预算上界"满足，非放宽 P0）。**诚实降级替代拒跑**：FMP grades 已定 advisory（2026-07-10）→ 配额耗尽 → grades 中性、run 照常出（research 模式），不 fail-closed 拒跑；仅 health-critical 源坏才 NO-EMIT。auto-derive 仅 paper 一键默认（代码 gate 到 executing paper run），显式 `--pass2-call-budget` 精确路径保留供其它受控用途；不授权 provider selection/storage/全 universe 之外新边界，SR-PROVIDER-001 其余门不动。**去掉天花板理由**：`top_k`(≤250) 已结构性把 grades 压在免费日上限内（天花板冗余）；grades 为 advisory→耗尽应中性照跑而非拒跑（天花板会误挡）；去掉消除"卡 grades vs 卡全部调用"的实现歧义、更简单、零一键破坏风险（2026-07-22 用户定：留天花板须不破坏一键零额外操作，否则去掉）。
- **Boundary**: 只改一键授权流（capstone runner/ps1 的预算门 + config），不改选股 / PIT / scoring / veto / secret；不新增 provider 调用类型；不放宽 §18.0 P0（standing cap 本身仍是显式用户授权、只是一次性预授权一个上限）。
### R-ASHORT-P5-EXECUTION-CONTRACT-GAPS - industry-weight adjudication would compare the wrong watch pool, condition away zero-effect weeks, and compete with v2 for a hard provider-call budget

- **Status / severity**: **in_progress — P5a is complete and independently reviewed but has no live evidence; the shared-cache extension (including F1 migration and Obs2 plan-window repair) remains comparison-only** (P1 comparison-evidence integrity and operability; current production ranking unchanged). The extension must preserve P5's v2-first/P5-second budget contract and cannot start any clock itself.
- **R-ASHORT-P5-WATCH-POOL-SEMANTICS**: the former `_profile_top_n` remains human-readable comparison output only. `engine/egs_industry_heat.py::select_profile_watch_pool` now owns the formal Tier1-only ordering, L2-overflow cap, sequential L1/L2 concentration checks, short-pool behavior, and Top15 truncation; `A-EGS/egs_main.py` uses it for both `top50` and formal `watch_df`, while all four profile lists write it as `profile_watch_pool_top15`. A direct invariant test pins the active balanced profile's member order to the same frozen-universe formal pool. This selector is not yet a P5 evidence clock or a Stage3 Top5 selector.
- **R-ASHORT-P5-CONDITIONAL-SAMPLE-BIAS**: P5 currently labels identical-list weeks `no_count` and excludes them from the 12/24/36 primary effect series. That answers “when the recipes happen to differ, which picks win”, not “what is the whole-policy effect of replacing the recipe”; identical arms are valid policy weeks with paired effect exactly zero. Required repair: freeze two clocks before the first record: (1) all identity-valid/mature `eligible_policy_weeks`, including identical lists with zero paired difference, drive the primary whole-portfolio effect and formal checkpoints; (2) `difference_weeks` remain a diagnostic/minimum-information gate and drive the changed-name sensitivity analysis. Reserve `no_count` for missing/invalid/identity-conflicted evidence, and define its denominator explicitly. Apply the same correction to P4's whole-policy adjudication.
- **R-ASHORT-P5-CACHE-BUDGET-STARVATION**: P5a established the only provider writer, v2 fixed reservation, P5 deterministic overflow deferral, and P5 capture/settle/progress. This review-pending extension makes the same atomic cache also serve P2/P3: it first reads the existing cache, keeps the 91-call ceiling, schedules v2 first, then P5, then P2/P3, and records per-consumer deferrals. It writes one raw `stocks/limits` surface plus a deterministic P2/P3 execution projection; `weekly_screening.ps1` passes that exact file to all four tracks, whose ledgers/verdicts remain independent. It changes neither official M6.7 nor any P5/P2/P3 production decision, and no ceiling increase is authorized.
- **R-ASHORT-P5-ADJUDICATION-PLACEHOLDERS**: the capture omits the market-regime label required by the 24-week diversity gate; `风险门没有恶化`, `关键风险明显恶化`, and H5/H20 `明显反向` have no frozen numeric definitions; `no_count <=20%` has no denominator; and drawdown is not labeled as close-based. Required repair: either remove the regime-diversity gate or freeze its PIT source/fingerprint in every capture; give every early/formal/reversal/risk predicate an exact formula and threshold; define the no-count opportunity set; and label drawdown as close-based portfolio NAV drawdown (not intraday drawdown). Reuse of v2 helpers is allowed only where their input/statistical semantics match this fixed-slot portfolio contract.
- **Cross-track closure**: P4 must fingerprint the exact upstream `active_profile` and official pool selector. Any profile/selector change retires that P4 epoch. An overlay is necessarily evaluated on an upstream profile, so governance should bind the tested pair `(active_profile, overlay_contract)` rather than claim the layers can be literally independent. Separate P4/P5 winners may not be combined without a newly frozen combined-policy experiment. Engineering may establish the shared selector/P5 capture foundation before P4, but P4 Cut1 need not wait 24/36 weeks for P5's terminal result; P4 Cut2 remains independently evidence-, review-, and user-gated.
- **Closure condition**: revise the final P5 (and affected P4) desktop contracts, then re-review against the live selector/cache code. P5 execution may begin only after the four Required contracts above are explicit and tested; formal forward time starts only on the first post-PASS live canonical capture with valid identities and available observed-adjustment data.
- **Design repair 2026-07-19; P5a implementation/PASS 2026-07-22; shared-cache P2/P3 extension review-pending 2026-07-22**: desktop `todo_ashort.md` uses the exact production watch-pool selector contract; separates `eligible_policy_weeks` from `difference_weeks` and counts identical lists as zero whole-policy effect; freezes missing-row and deterministic cache budgeting under the existing 91-call ceiling; removes market regime as a hard gate and gives no-count/drawdown/reversal/risk predicates exact meanings. P5a freezes source-bound four-profile private captures only after the complete official weekly bundle, settles H5/H10/H20 only from the observed-adjustment cache, writes de-identified public counts/reminders, and defers P5 after v2. This extension retains that order and adds P2/P3 only after P5. P5b adjudication, every production/profile change, and any real capture remain absent.
- **R-ASHORT-P5-SHARED-CACHE-1.1.0-MIGRATION (F1, resolved by self-reviewed implementation)**: `daily_cache.json` schema `1.1.0` requires `stocks[].high/low/vol/suspended` + `limits[].down_limit` + `rows`; a validated on-disk `1.0.0` cache has none of those execution fields. `_load_existing_cache` now treats exactly `1.0.0` as an empty `1.1.0` in-memory cache: it does not merge, backfill, or trust legacy rows, and re-accumulates only the currently frozen consumer windows within the existing 91-call budget. The original file remains byte-identical until the fully rebuilt `1.1.0` payload has passed schema validation and the existing atomic writer replaces it. Focused regression proves stale rows are not retained; the reverse probe makes the provider fail and proves the legacy file remains unchanged. This is comparison-only and does not alter M6.7/V14.3/overlay/production.
- **Obs2 (resolved by self-reviewed implementation)**: `corporate_action_verified` remains hard-wired `False` (`_provider_rows_for_symbol:421`, never set True), and the cache continues to null the OHLC of each unverified adj-factor-change day. Managed-exit v1.1.0 now slices before OHLCV normalization: it retains the exact reference bar, the 15 immediately preceding completed rows needed by ATR14, and the first 20 rows after the frozen decision date. A null/invalid row inside that required window still fails closed as `no_count`; an older or later cache row cannot poison an unrelated plan. P2's source/version fingerprint and P3's source-file fingerprint both therefore start a new private evidence epoch rather than recomputing old evidence. Focused direct, P2, and P3 regressions cover the outside-window settle path and the retained-window no-count path. It remains comparison-only and does not affect selection/real-money.

### R-ASHORT-D4-SOURCE-HASH-BINDING-UNENFORCED - D4 ablation claims source-hash binding it never enforces

- **Status / severity**: **route-b relabel VERIFIED; code PASS on independent Claude re-review (2026-07-16); committing; P2** (A-short D4 research-only; no production / selection / real-money impact; the spent result is now explicitly reviewer-trust-only rather than falsely source-bound).
- **Re-review verdict (2026-07-16)**: route (b) taken and verified — result `binding_status="posthoc_recorded_unverified"` is schema-`const`-pinned (the runner can no longer emit a result claiming any binding), `rank_samples_sha256=d04ef86a…` and `row_count=360` are unchanged, and the overclaim is corrected in the docstring, ledger spend-log (×3) and result. The line-50 "source-hash-bound" phrase is retained only as the mandatory contract for a FUTURE D4 (line-51 corrects the spent result). The two low Optionals (`_bool` numeric parse, write-before-spend atomicity) are unaddressed but moot for the already-spent singleton (no re-run); carry them to any future enforced-binding D4.
- **Risk**: `runners/a_short_d4_policy_ablation.py` docstring (L1) and the spent ledger spend-log (L195) call the study "source-hash-bound", and the closed-loop standard requires the input `rank_samples.csv` SHA-256 to be preregistered and unreplaceable. In fact the preregistration `source_contract` freezes only the 9 required column names — there is no expected-SHA field, and the preregistration schema `additionalProperties:false` forbids adding one. The runner merely RECORDS `_sha256(samples_path)` (L160); it never compares it to a frozen expected hash, and `--samples` (L206) is an arbitrary path with no path const. Any CSV carrying the 9 columns is accepted and its own hash recorded. The singleton ledger is already spent (`tests_spent_count:1`, `planned_tests:[]`) and no `rank_samples.csv` exists anywhere in the repo, so the recorded `rank_samples_sha256=d04ef86a398e02fd6c4289582e38569c11b4cd84b1cc7b55a63fedf3db675532` (row_count 360) cannot be checked against any reference — the result's provenance is unverifiable and the "unreplaceable / source-hash-bound" contract is unmet. Sibling runners in this repo enforce an `expected_sha256` comparison; D4 omits it.
- **Required**: Either (a) redo D4 under a NEW reviewed preregistration + new singleton ledger + user approval that freezes the expected `rank_samples.csv` SHA-256 BEFORE running, with the runner enforcing `_sha256(samples)==expected` (raise on drift) and pinning/validating the samples path; or (b) correct the overclaim — change the runner docstring, ledger spend-log and closed-loop standard to state the input hash is RECORDED post-hoc, not pre-bound, label the current result's provenance reviewer-trust-only, and require enforced binding for any future D4. The artifact must not remain PASS-able as "source-hash-bound" as written.
- **Optional (low)**: `_bool` (L57-58) maps a numerically-stored `hard_veto` of `1.0` (str→"1.0") to NOT-vetoed, unlike the analyzer's `_parse_bool` which accepts numeric 1.0 — a possible pre-veto population skew if the CSV stores hard_veto numerically; align the parser. Also `execute()` writes the result (L188) before marking the ledger spent (L200) with no temp-then-rename/try-finally — a failure in between leaves an emitted result with an unspent (re-runnable/overwritable) ledger; currently moot because the ledger is already spent.
- **Closure**: Claude verifies either the preregistration+schema carry a frozen expected input SHA and the runner fails closed on mismatch (with positive + negative control tests), or every "source-hash-bound / unreplaceable" claim is corrected to match what is actually enforced and the current result is labelled unverified-provenance.

- **Repair update (2026-07-16; route b, pending review)**: No historical preregistration, source snapshot, head metric, result value, or ledger spend count was changed. The runner and the spent ledger now say only that the SHA-256 was recorded post-hoc; the output schema/result are v1.1.0 and const-pin `input_integrity.binding_status="posthoc_recorded_unverified"`. The current result is therefore reviewer-trust-only and cannot be cited as source-hash-bound. Any future D4 needs a new reviewed preregistration and singleton ledger that pin both canonical input path and `expected_sha256` before execution, with the runner rejecting any mismatch before an output or ledger spend.
- **Optional repair (2026-07-16; code PASS on independent Claude review)**: future execution now treats numeric `hard_veto` 0/1/0.0/1.0 like the Rule6 parser, atomically commits the spent ledger before publishing the result, and removes any temporary file. A late result-publish error is therefore fail-closed as “budget spent, no official result”, never “result exists but budget unspent”. The already-spent historical result was not rerun or modified. Verified: `_bool` positive/negative control (numeric 1.0→veto, 0.0/false→not), `_commit_spent_result` order test asserting ledger-written-before-result under a publish failure, and atomic-write no-temp-residue test; D4 focused OK, full a_short pack 1490 with only the 3 pre-existing entry_funnel fixture ERRORs, no regression.

### R-ASHORT-FACTOR-COMPARISON-CLOSED-LOOP - per-factor comparison needs an executable weekly evidence and reminder loop

- **Status / severity**: **open; P2** (A-short only; v2 Knife 1 private evidence and Knife 2 offline adjudication are independently reviewed and committed; Knife 3 is the comparison-only weekly integration and requires independent review before any interpretation or production proposal). Independent Claude review 2026-07-16: D2 comparison recorder = code PASS; D4 ablation raised `R-ASHORT-D4-SOURCE-HASH-BINDING-UNENFORCED` (the "D4 source-hash-bound" clause below is the intended standard, not yet enforced).
- **P0 date-contract/cache execution (2026-07-19; Codex, uncommitted; independent review pending)**: the live wrapper now removes retired v1 write flags/settler, runs a bounded private v2 cache builder before M6.7, then passes only v2 root/cache/forward. The builder reads frozen selected unions only, has a pre-fetch endpoint-call cap with one attempt per endpoint, writes atomically beneath the gitignored v2 root, preserves old cache on conflict/failure, records missing adj factors as `provider_missing`, and skips terminal captures. Capture/source receipt schema now bind `run_date`, `source_as_of`, `price_data_through`, candidate digest and published M6.7 digest. Forward canonical capture permits weekend/holiday preparation for the next canonical trading day only when real local run date, official `intraday_prior_settled` lineage and prior-settled price binding all agree; frozen price series/settlement close use `price_data_through`, while entry remains canonical T+1. Any cache/capture failure remains non-blocking; a changed same-decision replay is not overwritten and emits a fixed frozen-evidence warning. Focused core/cache/date tests, effect-contract and v2 pipeline tests passed; no live provider/weekly run was made. The 600s broad `test_a_short*` discovery did not finish and emitted `E` markers, so it is not closure evidence; full dynamic guardrails also need a Python with required A-short dependencies. **This does not close the P2 risk or authorize production interpretation/switching.**
- **P0 independent review (2026-07-19; Claude Code → PASS; committed 3500)**: full-slice adversarial review — engine/adapter/pipeline/ps1/cache_build integral read + finalized B-contract conformance. Date identity matches B: `decision_date==source_as_of` (canonical Monday, `weeks/<decision_date>` freeze key) / `run_date`=real local day / `price_data_through`=last settled bar; forward eligible only on live-canonical (`decision_date>=run_date`) + official `intraday_prior_settled` prior-settled binding; BOTH the PIT gate and the settlement close-check re-anchored to `price_data_through`, entry stays canonical T+1 (Tuesday, matching forward_tracker/rank backtest). Anti-forgery holds: `run_date`/`price_data_through` are bound to the published M6.7 `price_freshness` lineage, plus `_today()==run_date` + `decision_date>=run_date` + published-bundle verification, and settlement double-gates `adj_factor_observed`+`provider_observed`. Cache builder honest: missing adj → `provider_missing` (never fill→observed), call-cap before fetch, atomic write, gitignored private root, no token leak, conflict fail-closed, terminal captures skipped, no future (>run_date) bars. Drift replay is not overwritten (fixed frozen warning); cache/capture failure never blocks or alters M6.7. Verify (3500 deps-complete env): 550 P0-surface tests OK (Ran 534 focused incl. weekly_pipeline+guardrails + 16 effect-contract hash-pin; includes the weekend→Monday canonical capture end-to-end test and the v1-flag/settler-removed guardrail); one worktree-isolated adversarial agent independently confirmed all three invariants (forward-forgery / cache integrity / production non-blocking) HOLD with no exploitable hole; full `test_a_short*` discovery = Ran 1706 OK (deps-complete env, ran to completion — zero regression in unchanged components); secret/scope/whitespace scan clean, no `state/` private cache tracked, no cross-market. **Non-blocking Optionals**: (2) RESOLVED — the adapter forward-lineage rejection paths (forged `mode` / `price_data_through`≠official `accepted_prior_settled`) now have negative regression tests (`test_forward_capture_rejects_forged_or_mismatched_price_freshness_lineage`; v2_weekly 6 OK, test-only). (1) documented residual — `a_short_factor_comparison_v2_daily_cache` open/close have no numeric bound; the naive schema hard-bound is deliberately NOT taken because one glitchy provider row would fail the whole cache write (worse availability than one skewed comparison-only row), the correct per-row builder degradation is not a small change, and the gap is comparison-only (cannot reach production; settlement `observed`/`provider_observed` gate already mitigates). Still comparison-only; does NOT close the P2 risk or authorize production interpretation/switching; cherry-pick to master and the first controlled live-canonical enablement remain separate steps.
- **v2 knife-3 weekly integration (2026-07-18; Codex execution, not a production switch)**: weekly accepts only an explicit gitignored v2 root and a serialised existing cache beneath that root; it has no provider handle or fetch fallback. Before M6.7 build/publish it runs cache-only settle then adjudication and reads the freshly atomically rewritten private `reminder.json`; public JSON/Markdown/terminal receive only the same fixed de-identified status/count message. Missing cache/root, malformed private state, receipt/ledger drift or any validation failure yields `evidence_unavailable_or_inconclusive`, count zero and no old reminder. Only after JSON+Markdown+matching receipt publish succeeds does it verify the bundle bindings and freeze the current capture using the published JSON SHA-256. Publish failure cannot reach capture; capture failure is explicit/non-blocking and cannot retract M6.7. Legacy v1 capture flags fail before weekly work, preventing v1/v2 dual write/count. No EGS/TopN/M6.7 action-or-size/veto/account/production policy changes.
- **v2 knife-3 independent review (2026-07-18; Claude Code → PASS; committed)**: production-boundary verified. My own probe: 11 adversarial cache/private-state attacks (zero / huge / NaN / negative / null prices, empty stocks, duplicate-conflict rows, corrupt & non-JSON ledger, corrupt capture) ALL made `settle_and_summarize_v2_weekly` RETURN a summary — never raise — so the unguarded pre-M6.7 call at `runners/a_short_weekly_pipeline.py`~L3824 cannot block the production weekly run (bad prices → no_count → `evidence_current` count 0; integrity failure → `evidence_unavailable_or_inconclusive` count 0, no stale reminder). The §6a independent agent (PARTIAL — terminated on a session usage limit) independently confirmed the two structural invariants before dying: the pre-M6.7 call is unguarded in `main()` AND `factor_comparison_v2` is consumed ONLY by `validate_v2_public_summary` + the render/terminal banner, never by any scoring / selection / TopN / veto / sizing path; I completed the crash-reachability attack it had queued. Full A-short lane regression `discover -p test_a_short*.py` = exit 0 (zero failures — the §332 / tiering-rule-3a full run that Codex's 600s attempt timed out on). Verified cache-only (no provider), path-traversal guard, v1 write-flags SystemExit at entry (no dual write), public summary forced == canonical (no ticker / arm / hash / path / return leak; unavailable ⇒ count 0), capture only after `_verify_published_weekly_bundle` matches the published JSON/MD/receipt (no false forward week), forward flag still knife-1 real-local-date-gated. **Optional (non-blocking; defense-in-depth)**: the pre-M6.7 helper catches a specific exception tuple, not bare `except Exception`; no reachable uncaught path was found (design intends `ComparisonV2Error` for all integrity failures, all caught), but at this single production seam a bare `except Exception` would make the never-block-production guarantee immune to a future latent uncaught type in the 2000+-line settle/adjudicate code. → **RESOLVED 2026-07-18** (user-directed trivial one-liner; Claude self-implemented + self-verified, NOT an independent adversarial review): `settle_and_summarize_v2_weekly` now uses `except Exception` (BaseException — KeyboardInterrupt/SystemExit — still propagates). Behaviour-identical for every reachable path (`ComparisonV2Error`/`ValueError`/etc. ⊂ `Exception`); `py_compile` OK + focused `test_a_short_factor_comparison_v2_weekly` 4 OK; committed to 3b4b, not yet cherry-picked to master. **Residual (item 16b)**: a fresh COMPLETE independent §6a agent pass was not achievable this session (usage limit resets 19:40 SGT); the partial agent + my 11-case boundary probe + integral read cover the boundary — a full agent re-run is available for extra assurance.
- **v2 knife-1 update (2026-07-18; implementation complete, pending Claude independent review)**: a separate gitignored `factor_comparison_private/v2` now freezes D1/D3 question/explicit-arm capture, canonical Phase5 baseline parity, source receipts, three-part epoch identity, cache-only QFQ outcome/risk evidence, selected-union observed-adjustment coverage and question/arm no-count records. Forward status additionally binds real local date, exact run/source as-of, actual normalized candidate digest and exact-as-of candidate close; capture/receipt bindings are re-derived on replay and settlement. It imports no v1 evidence, calls no provider and does not alter weekly/EGS/M6.7 or issue any verdict/reminder; v2 adjudication remains knife 2 and weekly wiring knife 3. Full contract: `docs/a_short_factor_comparison_v2_design.md`.
- **v2 knife-1 independent review (2026-07-18; Claude Code — FAIL, one material Required)**: full-slice adversarial review (design + code 958L + 4 schemas + governance + 20 tests) → one blocking numeric defect `R-ASHORT-COMPARISON-V2-MAXDD-CUMULATIVE-COMPOUNDING` (`max_drawdown_pct` computed by compounding cumulative-from-entry returns) + three non-blocking Optionals; all integrity / single-factor-isolation / PIT / private-boundary invariants HELD under my own read+probes and one §6a independent read-only adversarial agent. Not committed. Full detail in the dedicated entry below.
- **D2 re-review (2026-07-16; code PASS + non-blocking Optional)**: the §6a independent adversarial agent plus my own read confirm the SANCTIONED runner path is non-forgeable — `forward_eligible = (decision_as_of == real-clock run_date)` is derived and re-validated (never settable), `save_action_records` binds the current row's run_date to `_current_run_date()`, `summarize_action_records` counts only `forward_eligible` rows (historical replays surfaced as `historical_not_counted`), and via the runner you get at most one forward_eligible row per real day (as_of must equal today, merge dedups on as_of), so 12 forward weeks require 12 real days. **Optional (defense-in-depth; comparison-only + downstream human-gated; non-blocking)**: the internal helpers (`build_action_record` / `save_action_records` / `refresh_action_records` / `_write_json`) do not self-enforce the clock, so a DIRECT caller bypassing the runner can mint a fabricated ready-gate (12 forward-eligible + 8 divergent settled) in a single real day, or `_write_json` an arbitrary file — this is inherent to any file-based research artifact. Codex's handoff claim "写入器不能伪造历史有效周" is accurate for the runner path but overstated for direct-helper misuse. Optional remedy for a future round: gate on distinct `run_date`s in `summarize` (defeats the same-day mint) and scope the non-forgeable claim to the runner path.
- **D2 Optional repair (2026-07-16; code PASS on independent Claude review)**: `summarize_action_records` now rejects duplicate forward-eligible `run_date`s, so a direct helper caller cannot mint the 12-week/8-h10 review gate in one real day. This does not turn a local file into a cryptographic trust boundary: arbitrary direct file mutation remains outside the runner guarantee, and no production action consumes this track. Verified: positive control (two forward records sharing one run_date → ValueError) and no false-positive on legitimate distinct-run_date accumulation (each runner day yields ≤1 forward_eligible row, so real 12-week accumulation always has distinct run_dates); the honest scoping (not a cryptographic seal) is accurate and accepted.
- **Risk**: existing A-short sidecar comparisons are separate tracks. Without one common factor registry, independent weekly result files, forward-only settlement, frozen verdict thresholds, and a persistent reminder, the system cannot reliably tell the user which single factor should replace the baseline or which head should be dropped.
- **Required implementation / review standard**: D1/D3 freeze baseline plus `entry_ma_pullback`/`entry_range_pullback` and `iv_step_down`/`iv_joint_stress` under the schema-pinned governance artifact, write only `state/a_short/factor_comparison_private/`, and settle only from the existing forward cache. Each live capture additionally freezes a PIT CSI300 realized-state summary for comparison-only state coverage and D3 joint-stress input; unavailable state must fail closed and never alter production. Historical evidence cannot count; all four frozen heads use non-overlap paired h10 blocks, sign-flip plus four-head Holm correction, bootstrap CI and persistent reminders. D2 separately records raw/effective V14.2 labels, epochs, frozen advisory actions, M6.7 digest/count and audited forward market outcomes; it never mixes legacy unknown-only records. D4 has one spent, source-hash-bound ablation budget and keeps the 4d/5d crash question in its separate forward cohort. Claude must verify all four boundaries; none inherits another slice's pass.
- **D4 correction**: the already-spent historical D4 result has a post-hoc recorded SHA-256 only; it is reviewer-trust-only, not source-hash-bound. "Source-hash-bound" remains the mandatory contract for any future separately approved D4, not a property of the 2026-07-14 result.
- **Closure**: Claude independently verifies the document's section 8 checklist, including independent per-head weekly results, one-factor isolation, PIT/digest binding, forward-only counts, four-way verdict coverage, persistent reminders, private-path enforcement, and an explicit user-confirmed production-change boundary.

- **v2 knife-1 repair update (2026-07-18; pending Claude re-review, not committed)**: `max_drawdown_pct` now measures the H10 cumulative NAV path directly (`NAV=1+return/100`, initial peak 1.0), with direct and end-to-end value regressions. The capture docstring now distinguishes revalidated hashes/source/pool from hash-bound frozen arm decisions. The common pool is explicitly post-non-IV immutable gates: IV-only candidates are recorded as `iv_policy_deferred`, while contraction is excluded before an IV reason can mask it. Settlement rejects a cache decision close differing from the frozen candidate close. Bad-name/tail metrics are filled-position-only and record their basis/count; unfilled cash remains separate. Pool/outcome materializer source joins the epoch contract, so later semantic edits cannot silently reuse an old epoch.

- **v2 knife-2 update (2026-07-18; implementation complete, pending Claude independent review, not committed)**: `engine/a_short_factor_comparison_v2_adjudication.py` now consumes only hash/source-revalidated private forward ledger evidence and writes only private adjudication, human-receipt and reminder artifacts. It freezes the 0--11/12--23/24/36-week phases, non-overlap H10 blocks, paired bootstrap/sign-flip/sign display/Holm, 24/36 alpha spending, REML+Hartung--Knapp epoch aggregation, simultaneous-winner rule and every governed risk/no-count gate. A recommendation remains human-gated; receipt fingerprints bind the formal checkpoint and current contract, stale receipts cannot clear current reminders, dormant or combined questions require a new forward batch with no historical backfill. It does not call providers, wire weekly, alter EGS/M6.7/sizing/vetoes, or change production.

- **v2 knife-2 Required repair (2026-07-18; pending Claude re-review, not committed)**: independent adversarial review found and the current diff repairs four material gaps. (1) The adjudication digest now binds only the frozen statistical/question contract, so a legitimate three-signature epoch change causes cross-epoch re-aggregation instead of permanent refusal; statistical-contract drift still fails closed. (2) An adopt now requires the current epoch itself to have at least `min_epoch_blocks`, positive direction and no current harm. (3) Every simultaneous finalist pair must have `min_nonoverlap_blocks` common H10 blocks before its confidence lower bound can choose a winner. (4) `experiment_batches.json` is now an enforced private batch registry: capture freezes its active id into every question and ledger entry, adjudication filters to that id, dormant reactivation records a new id before any evidence can count, and accepted combinations create a forward-only batch that cannot capture until a new matching question is pre-registered. No historical row can advance either new batch. The repair adds regression coverage for each failure mode and retains the no-provider/no-weekly/no-production boundary.

- **v2 knife-2 combination-chain repair (2026-07-18; pending Claude re-review, not committed)**: the pre-registered combination is now executable, not merely scheduled. A generated combination batch freezes the two accepted `{question_id, arm_id, receipt_sha256}` components; a new `combination_policy` question may claim it only when it names exactly those components (one entry + one IV). The v2-only composite materializer applies both frozen components without changing production Phase5/v1 code, and the usual private capture, cache-only settlement, ledger batch binding and adjudication all consume it. The new end-to-end regression creates that registry record, captures/settles the combination, proves the ledger batch id, and reaches adjudication. It also caught the real outcome field name `evaluation_exit_date`, now read by the adjudicator (with legacy fixture fallback only), so actual settled v2 output no longer fails before block formation.

- **v2 knife-2 combination-receipt repair (2026-07-18; pending Claude re-review, not committed)**: claiming a registered combination batch now re-reads private `decision_receipts.json` before capture. Every recorded component must be present exactly once, have a matching SHA-256, be `accepted`/`decision=accepted`, match its frozen `question_id` and `arm_id`, and have a question-id set exactly equal to registered `component_question_ids`; absent, pending or mismatched receipts/components fail closed. The strengthened end-to-end regression proves no receipt and pending receipt both reject capture, only matched accepted receipts advance through capture/settlement/ledger/adjudication, and a real old combination capture→outcome→ledger row becomes zero-count when the same question switches to a new forward batch (not merely a no-ledger fixture). The new combination question does not inherit old D1/D3 rows or an earlier batch of itself.

### R-ASHORT-B1-TRAILING-STOP-PREENTRY-HIGH - holding stop could read price highs from before entry

- **Status / severity**: **code PASS on independent Claude re-review (2026-07-16); committing; P1** (A-short holding advice only).
- **Re-review verdict (2026-07-16)**: `holding_levels` is now strictly fail-closed when `entry_date` is set — an invalid entry_date, a non-dict price row, a missing/invalid `trade_date`, a non-finite post-entry high, or zero post-entry rows all return a reject; it never falls back to the pre-entry 20-day resistance (only a position with NO entry_date and no manual stop uses resistance). Fixtures were updated to dated series; the 4 previously-failing gap_data_registry holding-disposition tests now pass, and the full a_short pack is 1486 with only 3 pre-existing entry_funnel fixture ERRORs (missing `result/a_short/20260622/overlay.json`; identical on committed baseline da767a2e), no new regression.
- **Risk / repair**: `_fetch_price_series` dropped `trade_date`, so the holding stop could not exclude pre-entry rows before finding a trailing high. It now preserves the date, and the holding-level calculation is regression-tested with an earlier extreme high that must not affect a later entry's stop.
- **Review note (2026-07-16)**: verified — the returned rows are already clean 4-key `{trade_date,high,low,close}` floats (not raw provider rows), so the only new downstream key is `trade_date`; `holding_levels` (committed) filters `trade_date >= entry_date` (entry day inclusive) and a well-formed holding always has the as_of bar as a post-entry bar, so the pre-entry-high `resistance` fallback is not reached; ripple clean (`_safe_candidate` tolerates trade_date, `compute_indicators` ignores it, no `additionalProperties:false` schema embeds price_series); regression `test_dated_post_entry_high_excludes_pre_entry_extreme` + shape assertion pass.
- **Closure**: verified date is preserved through the price-series consumer and the pre-entry extreme regression passes; remaining step is the coherent commit once D4 is resolved.

- **Repair update (2026-07-16; pending review)**: `holding_levels` now refuses a held position with an invalid entry date, an undated/invalid price row, or no post-entry bar; it never falls back to pre-entry `resistance`. The direct held-fixtures now carry real `trade_date` values. Breached R3/R4a controls create their high on/after entry instead of accidentally relying on the historical pre-entry extreme. The earlier Claude PASS predates this stricter current diff and does not cover it.

### R-ASHORT-RULE6-UNRESOLVED-CHECKS-NONBLOCKING - pending Rule6 checks could still produce a new-position recommendation

- **Status / severity**: **in_progress P1** (code corrected and local verification passed; closure requires independent review and a reviewed commit). **User decision 2026-07-14**: the interim all-`观察` consequence is SUPERSEDED — the contract/gate machinery is kept and reused, but rework the check dispositions per `R-ASHORT-RULE6-COMPLETENESS-AND-DTIER-DISPOSITION` (D-tier → `not_applicable` to restore `建仓`; wire the computable checks; yfinance probe). Do not commit the current all-block behaviour as the closed state.
- **Finding**: EGS exported the complete Rule6 inventory, but eleven checks were normally marked \`pending_data\` or \`pending_llm\` and the weekly normalization/Phase-5 decision path did not consume them. A candidate with unresolved Rule6 checks could therefore be labelled \`建仓\` as though those checks had cleared.
- **Repair / invariant**: \`engine/a_short_rule6_contract.py\` defines the single expected Rule6 inventory and classifies it as \`clear\`, \`hard_veto\`, or \`manual_review\`. EGS validates its emitted inventory, weekly normalization preserves \`event_risk.rule6_checks\`, and the M6.7 engine consumes the gate. Any failed Rule6 check is a hard veto. Any pending, unknown, absent, duplicate, malformed, or inventory-drift state is visible as Rule6 manual review and, for a flat candidate, forces \`观察\` with no plan or new-position recommendation. Existing holdings retain their holding-management path; Rule6 pending state does not manufacture a clear decision or permit automatic adding.
- **Verification / closure**: new unit coverage proves complete-clear, missing, pending, and failed inventory states; engine regressions prove pending -> \`观察\`, failure -> \`否决\`, and pending existing holding -> \`持有\`; weekly regressions prove the full Rule6 list and its pending status survive normalization and that a canonical pending input is rendered observe-only. \`tests.test_a_short_rule6_contract\`, \`tests.test_a_short_phase5_engine\`, and \`tests.test_a_short_weekly_pipeline\` ran **562 OK** locally. No provider call, account/private-state read, ranking/threshold/sizing change, broker/order action, or commit occurred. Do not close until independent review confirms the scope and verification.
