### R-ASHORT-HISTORICAL-ST-DELISTING-NONPIT - historical replay read present-day security names

- **Status / severity**: **in_progress P2** (code corrected and local verification passed; closure requires independent review and a reviewed commit).
- **Finding**: historical get_stock_list() selected a time-valid listing universe but retained stock_basic.name, which is a current name. A security that was ST or marked for delisting at the replay date and later renamed could evade L0's name-based ST/delisting exclusion.
- **Repair / invariant**: historical runs now query Tushare namechange and replace every universe name with exactly one row whose effective interval contains TODAY. Missing rows, malformed dates/names, or overlapping active rows abort the historical run instead of falling back to present-day names. Current runs keep the existing stock_basic path and do not make the new request. L0's existing ST/退市 name exclusion therefore consumes the selection-date name.
- **Verification / closure**: offline regressions prove a current normal name with an earlier active *ST name is excluded, an incomplete history aborts, and a current run does not call namechange. The focused EGS suite ran **47 OK / 1 dependency skip**; the combined repair pack ran **577 OK / 1 dependency skip**. No provider call, account/private-state read, ranking/threshold/sizing change, broker/order action, or commit occurred. Do not close until independent review confirms the scope and verification.

### R-ASHORT-WEEKLY-PRICE-TRANSIENT-RETRY - one transient price-provider failure aborted the whole weekly report

- **Status / severity**: **in_progress P2** (code corrected and local verification passed; closure requires independent review and a reviewed commit).
- **Finding**: _fetch_price_series() made one pro_bar call and immediately aborted the weekly report for a short-lived timeout, connection failure, or explicit rate limit.
- **Repair / invariant**: the price fetch now makes at most three attempts with 0.5s then 1.0s backoff, and only retries transport-class exceptions or explicit 429/rate-limit signals. Parameter, payload, date/PIT, freshness, and every other non-transient failure still abort immediately; exhausted transient failures still abort and never become an empty/observe-only report.
- **Verification / closure**: regressions prove timeout recovery on the third attempt, a 429 exhausts exactly three attempts, and a ValueError makes one attempt with no sleep. The combined repair pack ran **577 OK / 1 dependency skip**. No provider call, account/private-state read, ranking/threshold/sizing change, broker/order action, or commit occurred. Do not close until independent review confirms the scope and verification.

### R-ASHORT-TUSHARE-PRIVATE-ENDPOINT-RUNTIME-CONTRACT - endpoint pin depended on an unpinned private Tushare layout

- **Status / severity**: **in_progress P2** (code corrected and local verification passed; closure requires independent review and a reviewed commit).
- **Finding**: A-short declared an unversioned `tushare` dependency while EGS, rank backtesting, execution-price materialization, and the IV probe each depended on Tushare's private `DataApi` endpoint field. The IV/materializer paths rejected a missing field, but EGS and rank-backtest only warned and created a client with the provider default endpoint. A routine dependency upgrade could therefore disable the endpoint repair or change its behavior after client creation.
- **Repair / invariant**: `requirements-a-short.txt` pins the verified `tushare==1.4.29` runtime and directly declares `tzdata>=2022.7`, so the Windows `ZoneInfo("Asia/Shanghai")` market-clock dependency does not rely on pandas' transitive dependency graph without unnecessarily freezing routine timezone-data updates. `engine/a_short_tushare_client.py` is the single A-short initializer: it verifies that exact version, requires the private `DataApi` layout, pins and verifies the configured endpoint, then creates the direct-token client without `set_token`. Every A-short production initializer now delegates to it. A version mismatch, missing/private-field drift, inaccessible client layout, or a non-persistent pin raises before `pro_api()` can create a provider client.
- **Verification / closure**: the new offline contract tests first failed three ways: unpinned version passed, missing private field only warned, and the requirements/callers were not centralized. The later direct-`tzdata` contract test also failed before the direct dependency was added. After repair, wrong-version and missing-field fake runtimes both raise before `pro_api()`; static coverage guards all four production callers against direct private-field or direct-client use, and the market-clock test guards the presence of a valid direct `tzdata` requirement without freezing its version. The affected regression pack ran 536 OK; the direct-dependency market-clock/Tushare/resolver pack ran 23 OK, with no Tushare/provider call, private-state read, ranking/threshold/sizing change, A-long source change, broker/order action, or commit. Do not close until independent review confirms the scoped repair and verification.

### R-ASHORT-MARKET-CLOCK-HOST-TIMEZONE-DEPENDENCY - A-share business dates followed the host timezone instead of the Shanghai market clock

- **Status / severity**: **in_progress P1** (code corrected and local verification passed; closure requires independent review and a reviewed commit).
- **Finding**: canonical decision-date resolution, the weekly wrapper's `RunDate`, EGS current-date branches, and forward-tracker maturity used the host-local wall clock. On a non-China host, one UTC instant can straddle the A-share 15:00 close differently: for example, `2026-06-26T07:30Z` is Shanghai Friday 15:30 and must resolve past the Friday close, while a west-coast local wall time can still read Friday 00:30 and resolve as before the close. This could select the wrong canonical date, live/historical guard, current-only source path, or forward-maturity date. Offset-bearing provenance timestamps are not business dates and were not changed.
- **Repair / invariant**: `engine/a_share_market_clock.py` is the single source for A-share business time (`Asia/Shanghai`). The canonical resolver defaults to its Shanghai wall time; EGS uses its market date for `TODAY`, the historical-L3 guard, the current-only SW path, and the L3 current-date branch; `forward_tracker._today_yyyymmdd()` uses it; and `weekly_screening.ps1` converts `UtcNow` through Windows `China Standard Time` before deriving `RunDate`. Explicit instants passed to the helper must be timezone-aware, preventing a silent host-timezone assumption.
- **Verification / closure**: a new same-instant regression proves UTC Friday 07:30 is Shanghai 15:30 and resolves to Monday 20260629, while a naive explicit instant is rejected by the shared clock. `tests/test_a_share_market_clock.py` plus `tests/test_resolve_canonical_asof.py` ran 19 OK; forward/cache and cut-6-to-10 regressions ran 33 OK; `tests/test_a_short_weekly_pipeline.py` ran 437 OK. Python compile, PowerShell parsing, and a remaining-time sweep passed; the only remaining `datetime.now()` hits in EGS are offset-bearing generation/publication timestamps. No Tushare/provider call, private-state read, ranking/threshold/sizing change, P2 endpoint change, broker/order action, or commit occurred. Do not close until independent review confirms the scoped repair and verification.

### R-ASHORT-S3A-HOLDING-T1-RESISTANCE-BYPASS - actual holdings bypass the S3a structural-resistance take-profit rule

- **Status / severity**: **in_progress P1** (code corrected and local verification passed; closure requires independent review and a reviewed commit).
- **Finding**: Before this repair, `runners/a_short_phase5_engine.py::holding_levels()` excluded holdings with an `entry_date` from the structural-resistance branch. Actual holdings normally carry that field, so a valid `resistance > close` could not become their S3a `t1`; S3b R3 then reused the RR fallback as its manual `reduce_price`. This conflicted with `docs/a_short_holdings_s3_system_levels_design.md` §2.
- **Repair / invariant**: `t1` now uses `resistance` whenever it is above `close`, regardless of `entry_date`; otherwise it keeps the existing RR-floor fallback. Post-entry-high stop binding, cross-week ratchet, post-tick breach behavior, and manual-only execution are unchanged. S3b R3 continues to reference the resulting S3a `t1` rather than recomputing a price.
- **Verification / closure**: the two focused tests first failed with actual held-position values `71.17 != 72.0` and `22.67 != 12.0`, then pass after the one-line repair. Added coverage proves both the held S3a structural-resistance branch and its S3b `reduce_review -> reduce_price` consumer; the reverse held case `resistance <= close` still uses the RR floor. `tests/test_a_short_phase5_engine.py` is 118 OK. The S3b suite is now 170 OK after its stale breach fixtures were given dated post-entry highs and its ratchet fixture used `max(S3a stop, triggered breakeven)` as required by the current invariant. Do not close until independent review confirms the scoped repair and its verification.

### R-ASHORT-RUN1-SW-MEMBERSHIP-WATCH-POOL-ACCOUNTING - capped SW source degraded to 104 requests and a target cap was treated as a health minimum

- **Status / severity**: **#1 + #3 code PASS on independent Claude review (2026-07-14); no Required; on-data confirmation (first v1.4 EGS run) is the only remaining step** (P1 provider-shape/PIT integrity for #1; P2 health-classification accuracy for #3; A-short only).
- **Claude independent review (2026-07-14, no subagent, integral-read of the diff + own test runs)**: both fixes correct. **#3 (watch pool) — no selection change**: only `build_data_health` changed; the export `watch_df = top50.head(watch_n)` is untouched, `watch_eligible_count = len(top50)` so `actual == min(eligible, target)` by construction → the real 13-Tier1 case passes as `eligible_pool_exhausted` (shortfall 2, no false WARN); the old `watch_count<watch_n` warning is removed and only a real export drop (`actual<expected`) errors; `validate_data_health_consistency` recomputes the reconciliation from the health's own numbers and rejects forged `reason`/low-coverage-`pass`. **#1 (SW membership) — mapping equivalent by construction**: the current-date L1 fast path (`index_member_all(l1_code, is_new=Y)`) normalizes official `ts_code/l2_code → con_code/index_code` via `_normalize_sw_member_columns` and runs through the SAME `_apply_pit_window` (filter to valid L2 codes + in/out-date PIT) as the legacy L2 path, so it reflects the same authoritative SW L2 membership; it is accepted ONLY when every L1 group is non-empty, below the 2000-row cap, well-shaped, and total active ≥ `SW_INDUSTRY_MIN_ACTIVE`, else the whole fast path is discarded for the unchanged per-L2 history path; historical `as_of` never uses the current-only route; `sw_industry_membership` records source/coverage/groups/degradation. Tests I ran: sw+watch health + rank reconciliation + contract + suspend + board/holder-PIT + 刀 6-10 regression = **43 OK**; schema const `1.4.0`. No Required; no threshold/weight/ranking/Tier2-fill change. **On-data residual (Optional, not a defect)**: the L1 fast path is a new *fetch method* for current-run industry classification; the equivalence argument is strong (same SW data + same PIT filter) and it fail-closes on any doubt, but the first real v1.4 EGS run should confirm the fast-path membership + candidate/industry distribution are stable vs the pre-fix L2 path — the fix itself gates this to the next authorized run and forbids back-filling from the old run.
- **Finding**: the 2026-07-14 run called unfiltered `index_member_all`, received its real `ts_code/l2_code` shape at a capped 3000 rows, but the EGS path only accepted legacy `con_code/index_code`. It silently reduced that response to empty, tried another thin unfiltered route, and finally made 104 per-L2 history requests. Coverage happened to recover, but the fast path was broken and the selected source/degradation was absent from `data_health`. Separately, `watch_n=15` was treated as a minimum even though formal production intentionally exports only eligible Tier1 rows; 13 eligible and 13 exported therefore produced a false data-health warning.
- **Repair / invariant**: a current-date run now queries official `index_member_all` by L1 group with explicit `is_new=Y`, maps documented `ts_code/l2_code` to the internal member roles, and accepts the route only when every L1 response is non-empty, below the documented 2000-row per-call limit, well-shaped, and the final active map reaches `SW_INDUSTRY_MIN_ACTIVE`. Any row-limit hit, bad/empty group, low coverage, missing endpoint, or historical `as_of` discards the whole fast-path result and uses the original per-L2 history path; historical replay never consumes current-only membership. `data_health` v1.4 records source, active count, request-group count, cache/fast/fallback state, and fallback reason. The observation pool now reconciles `actual == min(eligible, watch_n)`: `13 eligible -> 13 actual` is `eligible_pool_exhausted` with shortfall 2 but not a data failure; `13 eligible -> 12 actual` is a publish-blocking reconciliation error. Formal runs still never add Tier2 merely to reach 15.
- **Verification / boundary**: focused adversarial tests prove official-alias normalization and one-L1 fast-path use; exact row-limit rejection plus reliable fallback; historical-PIT avoidance of the current-only route; 13/15 accounted scarcity; and unexplained export loss failure. EGS Phase 6 discovery currently runs 40 OK / 1 dependency skip. No provider call, account/private-state read, threshold/weight/ranking change, Tier2 production fill, broker/order behavior, A-long/US-lane change, or commit occurred in this repair. A later normal EGS run must supply the first real `sw_industry_membership` v1.4 observation; it must not be fabricated from the old run.

- **Status / severity**: **code PASS on independent Claude review (2026-07-14); no Required; real-data v1.3 emit is the only remaining confirmation (next authorized EGS run)** (P1 official-selection completeness; A-short only).

The reconciliation CSV joins the atomic publish set + manifest hash (刀 8), and `analysis_input.universe_summary` gains `after_l0_count` + L1/L2 exclusion counts. No Required. **Not independently re-run on live data**: the `1512=656+601+245+10` figure is the implementer's offline replay; the fix's correctness does not depend on it (the logic makes any real post-L0 breakdown visible and fail-closes on genuine loss), and a fresh authorized EGS run will emit the first v1.3 official product for on-data confirmation.
- **Finding**: the 2026-07-14 live EGS artifact reported `L0=1512` and `full_count=656`, but persisted neither stage counts nor terminal exclusion reasons. `data_health` then warned solely because `full_count < 1000`. That heuristic could not distinguish deliberate L1/L2 rules from a truncated provider batch, so 856 rows looked silently lost even though the artifact itself contained no proof either way.
- **Local diagnosis (no provider call)**: an offline replay using the exact 20260714 caches reproduced `1512 -> 656` and accounted for every row: 601 L1 industry-leader eliminations, 245 L2 crash vetoes, and 10 L2 ESP-quality/valuation vetoes; 0 margin-growth vetoes, 0 unclassified exclusions, 0 unexplained additions/losses. Cached financial coverage was 5528/5528 and every L0 row had daily-stats, daily-basic, and financial coverage. The suspected financial/factor batch truncation did not occur in this run.
- **Repair / invariant**: EGS now records every post-L0 stage and emits `rank_universe_reconciliation.csv`, one row per L0 symbol with `ranked` or an exact terminal reason. Expected exclusions are limited to classified L1/L2 rules; master/L3/L4/L5 row changes, added/duplicate/unaccounted symbols, an unclassified expected exclusion, incomplete L0 daily-stats/daily-basic/financial coverage, or <95% full-universe financial coverage abort before publication. `analysis_input.universe_summary.after_l0_count` and L1/L2 exclusion counts are populated. The current `data_health` schema is v1.4 (v1.3 introduced this rank reconciliation; v1.4 adds Run-1 #1/#3 source/watch accounting) and no longer treats an arbitrary `<1000` ranked count as evidence of bad data; the official manifest hashes the reconciliation CSV.
- **Verification / boundary**: new adversarial tests cover expected per-symbol accounting, exact L1 reason classification, critical-source truncation, unexpected scoring-stage loss, removal of the false `<1000` warning, and analysis-input L0/exclusion emission. EGS-focused Phase 6 suite is 35 OK / 1 dependency skip; focused reconciliation+suspend/schema suite is 11 OK. A provider-forbidden 20260714 replay produced 1512 detail rows = 656 ranked + 856 classified exclusions, with 0 unclassified. A second provider-forbidden temporary **formal publish** completed `analysis_input -> reconciliation CSV -> data_health v1.3 -> official_publish`: health/rank status `ok/pass`, 0 source failure/unaccounted, and both health outputs + manifest include the reconciliation artifact. No provider/live call, threshold/ranking-weight change, account read, broker/order behavior, A-long/US lane change, or commit. Broader `test_a_short*.py` discovery is not PASS evidence in the bundled runtime: 15 errors are `requests`-missing dependency failures, while 1 error + 3 failures are unrelated existing holding-rule assertions; this slice's focused suites remain green.

The source stage therefore intentionally produces `data_degraded_whole_week_no_count` until a validated private sidecar exists; it cannot advance any evidence clock or issue a real-factor recommendation. A later evidence producer must remain private, bind the event/reconciliation evidence to the decision window, and be separately authorized/reviewed. No manual receipt, inferred adjustment mode, or historical reconstruction may bypass this gate.

### R-USSHORT-A1-CORPORATE-ACTION-EVIDENCE-PRODUCER - A1 matured weeks must not count on an unverified adjustment flag

The stage summary is counts/status/digests only; ticker-bearing rows stay under private paths and an echoed key is redacted before raw persistence. No real provider call occurred.
- **Claude implementation review (2026-07-18): PASS → RESOLVED.** Independently verified the end-to-end fail-closed chain: `build_adjustment_evidence` emits a `confirmed/no_events` certificate only when every family is complete+exhausted AND there is zero in-pool in-window event, else `ambiguous/unknown`; the consumer `engine/us_short_forward_policy_outcome.py:345-353` independently re-runs `paper_performance_evaluability_from_offline_evidence`, maps any non-`evaluable` status → `data_degraded_whole_week_no_count`, and cross-checks the stored `degradation_reason` against the re-derived status (a forged private-week cannot count). So eventful / incomplete / over-page → no-count; only complete zero-event counts. Budget hard-capped (schema `pages_fetched`≤2 + `raw_page_sha256` maxItems 2, ≤4 HTTP, market-wide no per-ticker, `JsonHttpClient` 0 retry); capability minted only inside a `production_run`; `_write_once` immutable + reuse requires all-families-complete (no expand/backfill); api_key redacted from raw + de-identified tracked summary. One independent worktree-isolated adversarial agent + my own probes/reads confirmed no false zero-event on any hostile payload, no secret leak to tracked output, no budget breach, SSRF continuation solid, `_private_path` fail-closed via real `git check-ignore`. Design intent honored: `scope.corporate_action_reconciliation_claimed=False` (no reconciliation overclaim — the §7 rewrite point). Tests: my explicit run of the 3 new/modified modules = **88 OK**; full `test_us_short*.py` = **4510 OK**. **Optionals (non-blocking hardening; all fail-closed = crash/brick, never a false count, ~never with a well-behaved provider)**: (a) `runners/us_short_forward_policy_corporate_action_fetch.py:162` `parsed.port` raises uncaught `ValueError` on a malformed next_url port → aborts the run instead of `incomplete/unsafe_continuation`; (b) `:225` `canonical_sha256(wrapper)` (outside the `:232` try) raises on `NaN`/`Infinity` in a page payload → aborts, and the pre-hash raw write then bricks retries via drift → intended `incomplete/malformed_payload`; (c) an incomplete fetch writes `ambiguous` sidecars that permanently no-count that decision_date (reuse needs all-complete), reinforcing the rare-counted-weeks viability flag above. Recommend Codex harden (a)/(b) before the first real weekly call.

Proved by two new planted-failure subtests (`bad_port_continuation`, `non_finite_payload`) added to `test_malformed_duplicate_http_and_pagination_fail_closed`; `test_us_short_forward_policy_corporate_action_fetch` = 7 OK, normal paths unchanged. ### R-USSHORT-YFINANCE-GRADES-FEASIBILITY-PROBE-PENDING-LIVE-AUTHORIZATION - yfinance analyst-grade feasibility is proven worth building and the low-trust non-critical source path is implemented.

Tracked counts-only summary `docs/us_short_yfinance_grades_feasibility_probe_summary_20260710.json` (no ticker/raw/URL/secret; probe self-check + schema passed); raw stays gitignored. - **Implemented offline contract**: `runners/us_short_yfinance_grades_feasibility_probe.py` binds a fixed 44-symbol experiment to the 20260710 top-200: 12 FMP-402 small-cap names (including ABCL/YEXT/ZBIO/ZURA), 30 highest-momentum names from that cohort, and FMP-200 controls HOOD/MRNA. Default dry-run reads the existing local artifacts and prints the plan with zero import/fetch/write. Only `--confirm-user-authorization` may lazily import an already-installed `yfinance`, read both `Ticker.upgrades_downgrades` and `.recommendations`, pace requests, stop on 429/crumb, keep raw tables under gitignored `provider_samples/us_short_yfinance_grades_feasibility_20260710/raw/`, and write a schema-validated no-secret/counts-only summary.
- **Decision rule**: worth building only if the FMP-402 small-cap group has >=50% usable <=90-day upgrade/downgrade coverage and the fixed sample completes without a rate-limit/crumb failure. The summary records field presence (`Action/Firm/ToGrade/GradeDate`), freshness/future-date counts, pacing/first failure, and aggregate HOOD/MRNA cross-check counts; it contains no ticker names, raw rows, URLs, or secrets.
- **Resolution (knife A - offline source)**: `engine/us_short_yfinance_analyst_grades.py::resolve_yfinance_grade_actions` consumes injected yfinance `upgrades_downgrades` rows and emits the same resolved grade-action shape as `resolve_analyst_grade_actions`: `signals`, `records`, `provenance`, `excluded`, and `checked`. It maps yfinance `Action` only (`up` => up, `down` => down, all other action labels neutral), applies the 90-day PIT half-open window, excludes future grade dates, composite-deduplicates by ticker/action/firm/to-grade/date, firm-strip/casefolds, records CHECKED coverage, and fail-closes bad shapes/non-finite values without using brittle grade-string ordering.
- **Resolution (knife B - gated fetch)**: `runners/us_short_yfinance_grades_fetch.py` is a dry-run/default optional/lazy yfinance runner for reviewed Pass2 targets. It requires `--confirm-user-authorization` / `confirm_user_authorization=True` before fetch, never installs yfinance, fail-closes dependency-missing (including broken-install `ImportError`) / 429 / crumb failure to neutral/down resolved actions, removes duplicate rows using the resolver's date/firm/action/to-grade/from-grade identity before resolution, and neutralizes every `YFinanceGradesError` from the strict resolver rather than aborting. Resolver rejection writes a full neutral/excluded resolved-actions shape and a provider-down counts-only summary with a fixed non-secret reason, while preserving the rejected raw/source package only under gitignored paths. It writes raw/source packages only under gitignored `provider_samples/` or `state/us_short/`, and writes a counts-only summary with no ticker/raw/URL/secret. This slice added fake-client offline tests only and made no live/network fetch.
- **Resolution (knife C - non-critical integration)**: Batch5 source-packet/data_context prefer `yfinance_grade_actions_path` when supplied and do not merge/double-count FMP + yfinance grade facts for the same ticker; capstone adds a gated `yfinance_grades_fetch` stage before Pass2, and Pass2 skips the otherwise redundant FMP grades endpoint when that yfinance artifact is present (FMP remains the no-yfinance fallback). `engine/us_short_run_origin.py` records the stage without making it a required provider-health source. Missing/down/limited yfinance grades resolve neutral for scoring/risk and must never block emit or change the frozen formula.
- **Required boundary**: do not install yfinance automatically; treat it as non-official / ToS-gray and low-trust. A live invocation requires fresh explicit per-execution user authorization under SR-PROVIDER-001. Regardless of result, do not alter §4.2 scoring, provider health, emit policy, DataHub, provider selection, production storage, or ship-gate without a separately reviewed design/implementation decision.

### R-USSHORT-BATCH5-MOMENTUM-TOPK-NARROWING-MISSING - the design's Pass-1 momentum/relative-strength narrowing ("收窄到候选集 几百只") that caps the Pass 2 funnel to a bounded top-K is NOT implemented anywhere; the momentum producer is a bounded <=3-symbol sample and the engine percentile-scores whatever it is given with NO top-K/threshold/rank cut, so a real full-universe momentum run would score ~2404 candidates and the preflight's 250 free-cap gate would BLOCK the run (stall, no automated narrowing) — the funnel only "works" today because momentum is a hand-picked 3-ticker placeholder

- **Status**: **resolved** (Claude solo build+review+fix 2026-07-07, Codex out of quota — flagged for Codex re-review from ef3c43f4; committed this cycle). The shared pure `engine/us_short_pass2_funnel.py::select_pass2_targets` (top-K by score DESC then canonical ticker ASC, ∪ forced-holdings; 13 unit tests incl. 1000→top-200 + tie determinism, committed dc47a5d1) is now WIRED into BOTH the preflight `_pass2_target_universe` (new `momentum_top_k` param + `--momentum-top-k` CLI, default 200, recorded in the summary) AND the live runner `_rederive_and_verify_pass2_targets` (reads the SAME K from the reviewed preflight), so a real full-universe momentum run narrows to top-200 and the preflight is `ready_for_reviewed_live_execution` instead of blocking at the 250 cap. Both summary schemas gain `momentum_top_k` (int 1-250); the canonical tracked preflight summary was regenerated (target 3 unchanged since 3<200, K=200 added). Wiring tests: preflight narrows 3-scored→top-2 by score; runner fetches ONLY the top-2 and never the below-top-K ticker. **§6a independent adversarial agent** (worktree, black-box, told nothing of the fix): top-K math / fail-closed shape rejection / target_symbols-only-forgery-rejection all HELD; it surfaced ONE circular-K seam — the runner re-derives using `momentum_top_k` READ FROM the very summary it distrusts, so a forged wider K makes the re-derivation reproduce the forged (wider) target and the equality check passes. Judged **P2/CONTAINED** (NOT the agent's claimed P1): the operator's `--expected-total-call-budget` is an incrementally-enforced pre-fetch hard cap (`assert_endpoint_budget_available` before EVERY call), so a widened K either raises the forecast → rejected at `_load_ready_preflight` unless the operator ALSO authorizes the wider budget (= explicit authorization), or keeps the forecast honest → the fetch aborts at the cap before any injected ticker is fetched (the agent's 16-call repro required setting the operator budget to 16). FIXED anyway (defense-in-depth + the re-derivation is DOCUMENTED as independent, and per the design-intent-conformance standard): the runner now RE-ANCHORS the live-spend budget to the RUNNER-re-derived target count via mirrored `_SEC_TICKER_MAPPING_CALLS=1` / `_PASS2_ENDPOINT_CALLS_PER_TARGET=5` constants (cross-checked vs the preflight `_forecast_calls` by test), rejecting a forged wider-K preflight BEFORE any fetch unless the operator independently authorized the matching budget. Reverse test: forged wider-K + honest narrow forecast + honest operator budget → rejected pre-fetch (0 URLs, no summary). Full offline `*us_short*` green. (Was: open design gap — no top-K anywhere in the momentum→projection→preflight chain; a real ~2404 universe would block at the 250 cap. NOT a defect in existing code — a REQUIRED build before any real full-universe run.)
- **Severity**: **P1** (blocks the real free weekly run: without a top-K momentum selection the preflight fail-closes at the 250 cap for any realistic universe momentum, so the pipeline cannot narrow to the design's "few hundred"; no data/security/PIT risk — it fails safe by blocking, but the free run cannot proceed).
- **Scope reviewed**: `runners/us_short_batch5_momentum_price_source.py` (`MAX_SYMBOLS=3`; `_selected_symbols` requires a 1-3 list; `_build_projection` scores only the caller-selected <=3 symbols); `engine/us_short_momentum.py::momentum_block` (percentile-ranks the given pool, only gate is min-coverage, NO top-K); `engine/us_short_seam_momentum.py::project_momentum_block` (pass-through, no cut); `runners/us_short_batch5_full_candidate_projection_inputs.py` (merges the <=3 scored + neutral-fills the ~2401 rest, no selection); `runners/us_short_batch5_full_candidate_pass2_preflight.py::_pass2_target_universe` (takes all scored∩eligible + `within_cap = target_count <= 250`, a BLOCK gate not a selection). Design authority `docs/us_short_system_design.md` §2/§4.0 explicitly specifies "收窄到候选集（几百只）". Reviewer integral read of the producer + independent §3.5 agent both confirm no top-K/threshold exists anywhere in the momentum->projection->preflight chain.
- **Why material**: the design's Pass-1 momentum narrowing to a bounded few-hundred is the mechanism that (a) makes the funnel actually narrow and (b) sets N's cap (K) → which deterministically decides whether Step 3 (FMP splits/dividends -> Massive offload) is structurally needed (FMP load = 3K vs the 250/day free cap). Without it, a real universe momentum run stalls at the 250 gate with no automated way forward. It is on the critical path to the free 12-month run — so "how long until real data runs" now includes building this.
- **Design decision (Claude 2026-07-07, recorded for Codex re-review of thinking + code — user asked the design be documented before building)**: top-K must NOT go in the momentum projection / merge (my earlier "place it in the producer/projection" line was WRONG, corrected here). The momentum projection's `coverage` dispositions are a FROZEN schema-first contract (`engine/us_short_seam_momentum.py`: `scored` / `insufficient_history` / `insufficient_coverage` / `absent_from_pool` — all DATA-AVAILABILITY reasons, pinned by `docs/us_short_seam_momentum_binding_20260702.json` + conformance test). A ticker demoted by top-K HAS data and WAS scored, so it has NO honest disposition there — moving it into the projection's neutral-fill would lie / break the frozen contract. Therefore top-K belongs at the SELECTION layer = the Pass 2 funnel TARGET derivation, where "take the top-K by momentum score" is semantically correct and the momentum projection stays honest (all scored = scored). Implementation: ONE shared pure function `select_pass2_targets(momentum_scores, eligible, forced_holdings, top_k)` -> `sorted( top_k(scored∩eligible ordered by score DESC then canonical ticker ASC) ∪ forced_holdings )`, imported by BOTH the preflight `_pass2_target_universe` AND the live runner `_rederive_and_verify_pass2_targets`, so the two remain PROVABLY identical — preserving the `R-USSHORT-BATCH5-LIVE-RUNNER-TRUSTS-PREFLIGHT-FUNNEL-NOT-REDERIVED` hardening (the runner still INDEPENDENTLY re-derives and must equal the preflight target). K = 200 (user decision 2026-07-07), CLI-configurable; the 250 preflight cap stays a safety backstop (K <= 250). Forced holdings are ALWAYS included even if below the top-K (they must be re-evaluated). The momentum projection + `full_candidate_projection_inputs` merge are UNCHANGED. Effect: a real full-universe momentum run narrows to top-200 (never blocks), and the Step-3 (Massive splits/dividends offload) decision is deterministic from K (FMP load = 3K vs 250/day). Tie-break by canonical ticker makes it deterministic across weeks.
- **Required build**: add the shared `select_pass2_targets` (pure/offline) + wire it into the preflight + runner (mirrored via the shared function) + a `momentum_top_k` param/CLI (default 200) + tests: >K scored -> exactly top-K by score; deterministic ties (score tie -> ticker ASC); forced holdings always included even if not top-K; K respected; a realistic >250-scored universe narrows to top-200 and the preflight is `ready_for_reviewed_live_execution` (NOT blocked); the runner re-derivation still exactly equals the preflight target (no re-expansion). §6a independent adversarial agent required (modifies the hardened funnel target derivation). Keep it offline/pure; the real universe momentum PRICE fetch stays SR-PROVIDER-001 gated.
- **Closure**: a build slice implements the top-K momentum selection + tests, the preflight narrows a large scored set to top-K instead of blocking, full offline green; reviewed + committed. Then N is capped at K by design and the Step-3 decision is deterministic from K.

Massive free-tier docs finding (2026-07-07; Claude solo, public docs review at massive.com/docs/rest/... — NO provider/network data call, no API key): Massive's per-endpoint docs directly state tier availability. The FREE/Basic tier ("Included in all Stocks plans") covers the funnel's Massive-side needs — `/stocks/v1/splits`, `/stocks/v1/dividends` (corporate actions), `/v1/open-close/{ticker}/{date}` (daily prices, delayed), `/v2/reference/news`, ticker-overview. Analyst ratings + earnings are a PAID Benzinga add-on (`/benzinga/v1/ratings`, "Included in select Benzinga expansion plans"). **This CORRECTS the 2026-07-06 Massive-alt plan artifact's claim** that Massive has "no analyst-grade equivalent" — Massive DOES have analyst ratings, just PAID (Benzinga); the plan reviewed only the overview page and missed the partner endpoints (the committed plan JSON is left as-is; this note is the single-source correction). The free-tier rate limit is ~5 calls/min + delayed data per public search summaries, NOT authoritatively confirmed — the pricing page + account dashboard are JS-rendered / login-gated and unreadable by the offline WebFetch tool, so the user's dashboard is the authoritative source. Implications: (a) Step 3's splits/dividends Massive-offload + news + prices are free-tier-viable; (b) analyst stays FMP-grades (free) with Massive/Benzinga analyst as an alternative PAID option at month 12 (Reminder B); (c) the funnel's ~16 calls/week fits 5/min trivially with pacing, and delayed data is fine for the pre-open weekly clock. A live SR-PROVIDER-001-gated 12-call probe would only ADD per-user-key entitlement confirmation + exact rate-limit response headers, since the free/paid gating is already in the docs.

SR-PROVIDER-001 stays open; the live half of Step 3 stays gated.

Step 3 (Massive corporate-action offload) BUILT — offline half (2026-07-07; Claude solo build+review+commit): the funnel's per-target split/dividend fetches in `runners/us_short_batch5_full_candidate_live_source_packet.py` now go to Massive (`/stocks/v1/splits` + `/dividends`, `provider_id="massive"`, `massive_headers`) instead of FMP, so FMP per target drops 3→1 (grades only) = 200/day at K=200, honestly under the 250/day free grade cap; the split/dividend calls move to Massive's per-minute (not daily-capped) free tier. **NO new engine was needed (simplicity-first)**: the existing generic `_payload_shape_from_payload` already counts the Massive `{"results":[...]}` envelope, and the capture is count/shape-only by design (no reconciliation/parsing), so this is a pure provider swap. The runner-test fake client returns the probe-captured real Massive shape. **§6a independent adversarial agent** (worktree, black-box): all 6 invariants HELD (no FMP leftover / honest non-vacuous family-sum / FMP=grades-only / capture stays count-only with adjustment fields never consumed / no secret-URL-raw leak into summary, capture, or raw / fail-closed count-0 on malformed Massive responses / funnel+budget invariants unchanged); **NO P1/P2**, two P3 nits ACCEPTED by design: (a) count-only `event_count`=len(results) over-counts junk rows on a malformed response (intended shape-only semantic, not consumed for any decision, no crash), (b) the gitignored capture artifact isn't text-scanned but is secret/URL/payload-free by construction. ### R-USSHORT-BATCH5-LIVE-RUNNER-TRUSTS-PREFLIGHT-FUNNEL-NOT-REDERIVED - the Step 1 funnel invariant (Pass 2 target = momentum-scored∩eligible + forced holdings, within the 250/day free FMP-grade cap, neutral-fill excluded) is enforced ONLY in the preflight producer; the live source-packet runner TRUSTS the preflight summary's `target_symbols` + `fmp_grade_calls_within_free_daily_cap` without re-deriving them, so a forged/hand-edited preflight can inject a neutral-fill eligible ticker as a live Pass 2 target OR re-expand to the full 2404 / 12021-call loop — the exact regression `R-USSHORT-BATCH5-FREE-RUN-FUNNEL-DIRECTION` names as the design-FAIL — while the live summary schema const-pins `within_cap`/`neutral-excluded` to true, FORCING a false attestation instead of rejecting the violation

- **Status**: **resolved** (Claude self-`修复` per user direction 2026-07-07 + independent adversarial re-audit; committed this cycle). The live runner now independently RE-DERIVES the funnel target (`_rederive_and_verify_pass2_targets`: `sorted(momentum-scored∩eligible ∪ canonical(forced-holdings))`) and RECOMPUTES within-cap from the momentum projection + candidate artifact BEFORE any fetch, and REJECTS any preflight whose `target_symbols` / within-cap disagree; the summary's `pass2_target_universe` (incl. the const-true `fmp_grade_calls_within_free_daily_cap` / `neutral_fill_tickers_excluded_from_expensive_pass2`) is now BUILT from the runner's re-derived+verified values, not copied from the preflight. A new `--forced-holding-ticker` input mirrors the preflight rule. Reverse tests: a forged preflight injecting a neutral-fill eligible ticker into `target_symbols` → rejected before any fetch (0 URLs, no summary); a `within_cap:true` preflight whose re-derived target exceeds the (test-lowered) cap → rejected before any fetch. Independent §3.5 adversarial agent: all 4 attack goals (neutral smuggle / 2404-12021 re-expansion / false within-cap-neutral attestation / reach-fetch-on-preflight-disagreement) HELD; it flagged one non-canonical-key false-reject, judged SAFE-BY-DESIGN and NOT changed (the raw-key read fails closed BEFORE fetch, consistent with the all-canonical sanctioned pipeline + the raw-keyed `_target_scoped_projection`; canonicalizing only the re-derivation would instead fetch-then-fail — strictly worse). Full offline `*us_short*` 3770 OK, doc/route 60 OK. (Was: open — Claude 审查 FAIL; the expensive live boundary trusted the preflight's self-attested target/within-cap, so a forged preflight could inject a neutral-fill target or re-expand to 2404/12021 while the const-true summary flags forced a false attestation.)
- **Severity**: **P2** (design-conformance + honest-summary / anti-fabrication; **CONTAINED**, not an active/silent bypass: exploiting requires an operator to deliberately hand-forge the gitignored/tracked preflight AND pass `--confirm-user-authorization` AND explicitly type `--expected-total-call-budget 12021`; no live run has executed; the explicit-budget + separate-network-approval + SR-PROVIDER-001 human gates still stand. Material because the funnel invariant is enforced at the CHEAP preflight, not the EXPENSIVE live boundary where the calls are actually spent, and the const-true summary flags make a violation structurally un-reportable — a tracked artifact forced to attest "within free cap / neutral-fill excluded" even after a 2404/12021 run).
- **Scope reviewed**: `runners/us_short_batch5_full_candidate_live_source_packet.py` — `_load_ready_preflight` trusts `pass2_target_universe.fmp_grade_calls_within_free_daily_cap is True`; `run_...` sets `reviewed_target_symbols = preflight["pass2_target_universe"]["target_symbols"]`; `_candidate_subset_artifact` validates targets ⊆ eligible ONLY (a neutral-fill ticker IS eligible → passes); `_target_scoped_projection` routes a neutral-fill target into `scoped_neutral` rather than rejecting; `_build_summary(pass2_target_universe=preflight_targets)` copies the preflight block verbatim. `schemas/us_short_batch5_full_candidate_live_source_packet_summary.schema.json` — `fmp_grade_calls_within_free_daily_cap` / `neutral_fill_tickers_excluded_from_expensive_pass2` = `const true`. Reviewer integral read of the runner diff + independent §3.5 agent (reproduced BOTH forgeries: neutral-fill JPM fetched; a forged all-2404 preflight drove the fetch loop toward 12021, aborted at 61 calls by the agent's own safety).
- **Verified correct (NOT findings — HELD)**: the PREFLIGHT producer derives the funnel correctly (target = momentum-scored∩eligible + forced; canonical 3 targets / forecast 16; neutral-fill excluded; 12021 demoted to a const-pinned hypothetical; the 250-cap blocks readiness at 251). The Step 1b entitlement engine `engine/us_short_numeric_catalyst_entitlement.py` is correct (not-entitled 400/402/403/404 → skip → neutral; malformed-200 / resolver-reject → fail-safe neutral; numeric `_finite_number`/`_strict_int`, date strptime/fromisoformat, exact-str-key, unhashable membership all typed; no fabrication; pure in-memory, no leak path). No secret/URL/raw leak in the modified runner (agent end-to-end secret probe clean; raw gitignored). Grades stay free/day-1. The runner DOES re-check `selected_symbols == reviewed_target_symbols` and targets ⊆ eligible. The gap is SPECIFICALLY the missing independent re-derivation of the funnel SELECTION + within-cap at the live boundary.
- **Required repair** (offline, this runner + its schema): in `run_full_candidate_live_source_packet`, re-derive the expected Pass 2 target INDEPENDENTLY from the referenced momentum projection + candidate artifact — `expected = (momentum scored keys ∩ eligible) ∪ canonicalized forced-holdings` — and REJECT any preflight whose `target_symbols` differ; independently recompute `fmp_grade_calls_within_free_daily_cap = (grade_calls ≤ 250)` and REJECT if false or if it disagrees with the preflight; verify no scored-eligible target is neutral-fill-sourced before emit, so the schema `const true` flags are only ever REACHABLE on the truly-true path (verified, not merely asserted). Add reverse tests: a forged preflight with (a) a neutral-fill eligible ticker in `target_symbols` → rejected before any fetch; (b) all-2404 targets + `within_cap:true` + budget 12021 → rejected before any fetch. Do NOT change the (correct) preflight producer logic or the entitlement engine.
- **Verification evidence**: reviewer integral read confirmed the runner takes `target_symbols`/`within_cap` from the preflight and copies the summary block verbatim; independent §3.5 agent reproduced both forgeries end-to-end. Codex-reported full offline `*us_short*` 3768 OK; reviewer full-pack re-run deferred to the fix round.
- **Closure**: a 修复 slice adds the live-boundary funnel re-derivation + within-cap recompute + the two reverse tests, keeps full offline green; Claude re-审查 PASS then commits.

### R-USSHORT-BATCH5-FREE-RUN-FUNNEL-DIRECTION - free-12-month run uses the Pass1->Pass2 funnel (expensive Pass 2 only on the momentum-narrowed candidate set + forced holdings, NOT all 2404); analyst rating-direction free from day 1; splits/dividends candidate-offload to Massive; + two cross-LLM continuity reminders

- **Status**: **open** — direction settled by the user 2026-07-06 (chat), recorded here because Codex reads the register, not chat. Step 1 / Step 1b now have a Codex execute slice in repo state: preflight/live runner use `pass2_target_universe` instead of full eligible neutral fill, and an offline entitlement-aware numeric-catalyst seam exists. This R-ID stays open because Massive quota verification, split/dividend offload, end-to-end free weekly report, 12-month evidence accrual, and paid real-shape validation are still separate work. This SUPERSEDES the "fund the full 2404x5=12021-call live run" framing for the FREE 12-month period: the 12021 forecast was an artifact of running Pass 2 on all 2404 while offline momentum was neutral-filled, NOT a required budget.
- **Severity**: P2 (roadmap / cross-LLM continuity; affects whether the free run is built to fit free quotas and whether the month-12 paid wiring is remembered — no data/PIT/security/ship-gate risk in itself).
- **Direction (user decision 2026-07-06)**: (1) Free-period weekly run = the design section 2 funnel: cheap BULK momentum/relative-strength/theme ranks the full active universe for free (~20 grouped-daily calls/week, NOT per-symbol), then the EXPENSIVE per-symbol Pass 2 families (FMP grades, Massive news, SEC offering/submissions audit, splits, dividends) run ONLY on the momentum-narrowed candidate set (design "几百只", capped so FMP grades stay <=250/day) + the always-forced current holdings — NOT all 2404. (2) Analyst RATING-DIRECTION (FMP `grades`) is FREE (HTTP 200 non-paid, proven 2026-07-01 AAPL/MSFT/NVDA in `docs/us_short_cut5_pass2_feasibility_probe_summary_20260701.json`) and is used from day 1 — NOT deferred to paid, so the 12-month paper track validates the real strategy. (3) Provider split: `grades` stays FMP-only (Massive exposes no analyst-grade endpoint); `splits`/`dividends` are a CANDIDATE Massive-offload (leaving FMP paying only the 1 grades call/symbol) pending Massive free-quota + field-semantics verification.
- **Ordered next steps**: Step 1 (Codex, offline/free, biggest lever) is implemented in repo state: preflight separates full local score coverage from expensive `pass2_target_universe`, current canonical summary keeps `eligible_count=2404` but `target_count=3` and `forecast=16`, and the live source-packet runner consumes only the reviewed target symbols and target-scoped projection artifacts. Step 1b (Codex, user-directed 2026-07-06) is implemented as an offline entitlement-aware seam in `engine/us_short_numeric_catalyst_entitlement.py`: injected `earnings_surprises` + `analyst_estimate_revisions` endpoint packets resolve via `resolve_catalyst_signals` and project through `catalyst_block`; 400/402/403/404 statuses mark not-entitled and skip per-symbol work to neutral; malformed 200 payloads fail safe to neutral. No provider/network/raw/DataHub/production/ship-gate/broker/A-share path is authorized or executed by Step 1b, and real paid payload shape remains unproven. Step 2 (needs user network approval + `MASSIVE_API_KEY`) — run the existing 12-call Massive probe packet (`docs/us_short_batch5_massive_quota_access_bounded_probe_packet_20260706.json`) for reachability/shape/quota-header presence (see Reminder A). Step 3 (Codex, dep on Step 2) — if Massive is verified, offload `splits`/`dividends` FMP->Massive. Step 4 (Codex, free) — end-to-end free weekly report (paper-marked): the OFFLINE composition is now PROVEN by `tests/provider/test_us_short_batch5_capstone_offline_e2e.py` (real funnel via fake client, no network -> narrowed 3-target source packet -> existing batch5->batch4 bridge -> private PAPER `weekly_report.md`/`action_table.csv` carrying the offline honesty sentinel, no secret/ticker leak); the remaining Step 4 work is the GATED live run (real funnel fetch, needs network approval) and optionally a one-click orchestrator. Step 5 — operate 12 months, accruing paper evidence. Step 6 (month 12) — start paying FMP and run a real-shape validation pass before relying on paid numeric catalyst data; wider Pass2 coverage still needs reviewed budget/provider gates.
- **Reminder A (do NOT overstate the 12-call Massive probe)**: 12 calls prove endpoint reachability/entitlement (200 vs 401/403), response shape, and WHETHER Massive returns quota/rate-limit headers. They do NOT by themselves prove the total free daily quota. If Massive exposes quota headers -> quota is readable from them; if NOT -> get the quota from Massive's account/plan/pricing docs (a reading task, no API calls), and only as a last resort run a larger sustained probe until a 429 ceiling appears. The probe packet already const-pins `quota_header_absence_clears_full_budget=false`; a passing 12-call probe is NOT proof of sustained free quota.
- **Reminder B (month-12 paid analyst wiring is NOT automatic — the two analyst signals behave differently)**: (i) Analyst RATING-DIRECTION — FMP `grades` -> section 5.2 collective-downgrade -> `risk_downgrade` — IS fully wired (`engine/us_short_analyst_grade_risk.py` -> `runners/us_short_batch5_data_context.py` -> `engine/us_short_core_score.py`) and free; more volume needs only a regenerated reviewed preflight/budget artifact, NO code change. (ii) The FMP NUMERIC catalyst sub-sources — `earnings_surprises` + `analyst_estimate_revisions` -> section 4.2 catalyst `earnings_surprise_pct`/`analyst_revision_net` — now have an entitlement-aware offline seam (`engine/us_short_numeric_catalyst_entitlement.py`) that can neutral-skip not-entitled packets or project entitled normalized rows through `resolve_catalyst_signals` + `catalyst_block`. The actual paid endpoint real shape remains unproven and provider calls remain SR-PROVIDER-001 gated; month 12 still requires a small real-shape validation pass before relying on those fields.
- **Review criterion (user-directed 2026-07-06)**: any slice claiming to implement this free-run direction is reviewed for DESIGN-INTENT conformance, not only code correctness (per `AGENTS.md` section "Codex adversarial review standard" item 16c). A technically-correct slice that DRIFTS from this intent is a FAIL — e.g. regressing toward the superseded full-2404 / 12021-call run instead of the momentum-narrowed funnel; wiring the numeric-estimate catalyst WITHOUT the entitlement-aware skip (Step 1b); deferring analyst grades to paid; fabricating candidate/coverage; or ignoring Reminder A (probe != quota proof) / Reminder B (month-12 estimate wiring). The reviewer states the design-conformance verdict inside the fixed Verdict/Action + Verify labels.
- **Closure**: Steps 1+4 land the free funnel run (offline/free, reviewed per slice); Reminder A closes when Massive quota is determined (Step 2 + plan docs); Reminder B is addressed by Step 1b (builds the self-activating entitlement-aware seam) and finally closes at the month-12 real-shape validation. Until then this stays open as the free-run direction + the two continuity markers.

### R-USSHORT-BATCH5-PASS2-PREFLIGHT-PRICE-BASIS-STRPTIME-BARE-VALUEERROR - the full-candidate Pass2 preflight's `_build_summary` calls `datetime.strptime(artifact["price_basis_date"], "%Y%m%d")` unguarded, so a regex-valid-but-invalid-calendar price_basis_date (e.g. 20261301 / 20260230 / 99999999) raises a bare ValueError instead of the typed FullCandidatePass2PreflightError — the recurring "unguarded date parse → bare exception" fail-closed class; the candidate schema + validate_candidate_artifact only regex-check price_basis_date, so the runner's trust boundary doesn't guarantee what the bare strptime assumes

- **Status**: **resolved** (Claude re-`审查` PASS 2026-07-06; committed this cycle). Codex extracted `_format_price_basis_date` (requires str + len-8 + ascii + isdigit, then `strptime` in try/except → `FullCandidatePass2PreflightError` on an invalid-calendar date) and uses it at L272 before the summary write; forecast/coverage/path/auth/network unchanged. Reviewer reproduced: `price_basis_date` ∈ {`20261301`, `20260230`, `99999999`, `00000000`, non-ascii/wrong-length/non-str/`None`} → typed `FullCandidatePass2PreflightError`; `20260702` → `2026-07-02` (no over-reject). New regression `test_invalid_calendar_price_basis_date_raises_typed_error_without_summary`. Full offline `*us_short*` 3731 OK (canonical preflight unchanged 2404/12021), doc/route 60 OK, zero regression. (Was: open P3 — L272 bare `ValueError` on a regex-valid-but-invalid-calendar date; fifth same-class recurrence this session — cut① observed_at OverflowError, cut② exchange unhashable, cut③ paper-eval status/mode unhashable, the still-open P3 fold-in screen_status; reviewer flagged L272 in the integral read, independent §3.5 agent confirmed the upstream regex-only gap.)
- **Severity**: **P3** (whole-class fail-closed contract robustness, SAFE-direction + CONTAINED + LOW-realism: `_build_summary` L272 `datetime.strptime(artifact["price_basis_date"], "%Y%m%d")` raises a bare `ValueError` on a regex-valid (`^[0-9]{8}$`) but invalid-calendar `price_basis_date`. Fails closed — it crashes in `_build_summary` BEFORE `_write_summary_validated`, so NO summary written, NO network, NO partial artifact; reachable only via a hand-edited/mis-produced gitignored local candidate artifact (the real `us_short_universe_fetch` producer parses `price_basis_date` via strptime, so a genuine pipeline artifact cannot carry a nonsense date); ungraceful exception TYPE only. Neither the candidate schema `schemas/us_short_universe_candidate_artifact.schema.json` (regex-only) nor `validate_candidate_artifact` calendar-validates `price_basis_date`).
- **Scope reviewed**: `runners/us_short_batch5_full_candidate_pass2_preflight.py` `_build_summary` L272; upstream `runners/us_short_universe_fetch.py::validate_candidate_artifact` (regex-only on price_basis_date). Independent agent reproduced bare `ValueError` for `price_basis_date` ∈ {`99999999`, `20261301`, `20260230`, `00000000`}. The sibling `_valid_observed_at` L127 correctly wraps `fromisoformat` in try/except → bool; the L272 strptime did not inherit that guard.
- **Verified correct (NOT findings — HELD)**: NO network / stays local (only `validate_candidate_artifact` [pure] + a local `git check-ignore` subprocess; no provider/HTTP call reachable); honest forecast / no overclaim (status `blocked_missing_local_inputs` on 3/2404 coverage, forecast 1+N*5=12021, safe scope/prohibited_claims/execution_gate flags hardcoded + schema `const`-pinned, unflippable by input; benchmark-padding/duplicate-canonical → still blocked/typed); tracked-summary hygiene (only bools/counts/repo-paths/public tickers; `_assert_text_safe` denylist + schema; the `payload`/`request_url` a naive grep hits are the attestation FIELD NAMES `tracked_summary_contains_raw_payload:false` etc., not content); path confinement (inputs gitignored `state/us_short` via real `git check-ignore`, summary canonical-docs-or-gitignored-provider_samples, auth gate, `generated_at` validated); schema enforced at runtime + floors match builder. ### R-USSHORT-BATCH5-PAPER-EVAL-OFFLINE-EVIDENCE-NOT-SCHEMA-VALIDATED - the new offline paper-eval gate consumes a "schema-bound" corporate-action evidence packet but never validates it against us_short_paper_eval_adjustment_evidence.schema.json at runtime; its weaker inline checks leave three gaps — unhashable dict/list status/mode → bare TypeError (recurring class), an omitted scope block skips the offline/no-ship-gate safety attestation the schema requires, and the const identity fields are unvalidated

- **Status**: **resolved** (Claude re-`审查` PASS 2026-07-06; committed this cycle). Codex applied the root fix — `derive_adjustment_context_from_offline_evidence` now calls `_validate_offline_evidence_schema(evidence)` (jsonschema `Draft7Validator` against `us_short_paper_eval_adjustment_evidence.schema.json`, raising `PaperEvalGateError` on any error) at the entry, BEFORE the inline derivation, so all three gaps fail closed: dict/list `status`/`mode` (schema string enum) → typed `PaperEvalGateError`, omitted `scope` (schema-required) → rejected, const identity/date-shape drift → rejected; confirmation-bit + ship-gate logic unchanged (inline checks retained as defense-in-depth). Reviewer reproduced: dict/list `status`/`mode` across adjustment_mode/split_handling/dividend_handling/ex_date + missing-required → typed `PaperEvalGateError` (no bare `TypeError`); the schema-validation helper fail-closes (errors → `PaperEvalGateError`). New regression tests `test_schema_validation_rejects_unhashable_status_and_mode_as_typed_error` / `test_schema_validation_requires_scope_block` / `test_schema_validation_rejects_identity_and_decision_date_shape_drift`. Full offline `*us_short*` 3723 OK (valid evidence still evaluates — no over-reject), doc/route 60 OK, zero regression. (Was: open P3 — engine claimed schema-bound but never validated the schema at runtime; the bare-TypeError sub-gap was the fourth same-class recurrence this session — cut① observed_at OverflowError, cut② exchange unhashable, the still-open P3 fold-in screen_status.)
- **Severity**: **P3** (SAFE-direction on the confirmation/ship-gate axis but a real schema-bound-vs-runtime mismatch on a §18.0 P0 corporate-action gate; each sub-gap fails closed on the axis that matters [bits can't be false-confirmed, `full_size_ship_gate_allowed` stays const False], but: (a) recurring fail-closed class — unhashable dict/list `status`/`mode` → bare `TypeError` not `PaperEvalGateError`; (b) skippable safety attestation — omitting `scope` bypasses the offline/no-provider/no-ship-gate scope block; (c) unpinned identity — wrong `schema_name`/`schema_version`/`decision_date` accepted).
- **Scope reviewed**: `engine/us_short_paper_eval_gate.py` `derive_adjustment_context_from_offline_evidence` L217-242 + `_adjustment_mode_confirmed` L160 / `_handling_confirmed` L174 / `_ex_date_consistent` L193 / `_optional_dict`+scope L226-233; `schemas/us_short_paper_eval_adjustment_evidence.schema.json`. Reviewer integral read + own whole-class probe + independent §3.5 main-tree read-only agent.
- **Technical finding**: the derivation does NOT jsonschema-validate the evidence (no `Draft7Validator` call); inline checks are the sole runtime authority and are weaker than the schema. (a) L165 `status not in _ADJUSTMENT_STATUSES`, L169 `section.get("mode") in _ADJUSTMENT_MODES`, L179 `status not in _HANDLING_STATUSES`, L198 `status not in _EX_DATE_STATUSES` hash the unguarded evidence value; reviewer reproduced dict/list `status`/`mode` on adjustment_mode/split_handling/dividend_handling/ex_date → bare `TypeError` (invalid-str → typed `PaperEvalGateError`, empty → all-False fail-closed). Schema pins `status`/`mode` to string `enum`s, so schema validation rejects these. (b) `_optional_dict(evidence,"scope",...)` returns `{}` when scope is absent → the `if scope and (...)` safety block L227 is skipped; schema `required` includes `scope` (const-true `offline_detection_only` + const-false provider/reconciliation/ship-gate flags). (c) engine checks only that top-level keys are known (closed-world L225) but never the `const` `schema_name`/`schema_version`/`decision_date` the schema pins. Independent §3.5 agent confirmed all three + that the confirmation bits (source_ref_id-bound) can't be false-confirmed and the ship-gate constants are unreachable by input.
- **Verified correct (NOT findings — HELD)**: NO fail-open to false-confirmed (each bit needs matching status enum + non-empty `source_ref_ids` ⊆ declared hashed `source_refs`; `events_reconciled` needs real events; ex_date `consistent` needs `checked_ids == event_ids` exact-set); ship-gate invariant airtight (`full_size_ship_gate_allowed` const False + `ship_gate_evidence_level` const across all 5^3 bit combos + injected-key closed-world reject); source-ref/path confinement solid (`_valid_source_path` rejects URL/absolute/`..`/drive/backslash/non-json; id/sha256 format + dedup); pre-existing `paper_performance_evaluability` hashable-safe (`is not True`); offline no-network no-leak. - **Required repair** (offline, this engine only): make the "schema-bound" claim TRUE — validate the evidence against `us_short_paper_eval_adjustment_evidence.schema.json` at the entry of `derive_adjustment_context_from_offline_evidence` (mirror the sibling `validate_forward_universe_snapshot` jsonschema pattern), which fail-closes all three gaps at once (schema pins `status`/`mode` string enums → dict/list rejected typed; schema requires `scope`; schema pins const identity). If jsonschema-at-runtime is not chosen, the minimal equivalent is: add an `isinstance(x, str)` short-circuit before EACH of the 4 status/mode membership tests (mirror `engine/us_short_status_source.py:454`), REQUIRE the scope block (reject omitted scope), and validate the const identity fields. Do not weaken the confirmation-bit or ship-gate logic.
- **Verification evidence**: reviewer reproduced dict/list `status`/`mode` → bare `TypeError` across all 4 sites (+ invalid-str typed, empty fail-closed) + whole-class grep (L165/169/179/198) + schema grep (status enums / required scope / const identity); independent §3.5 agent reproduced the same + the omitted-scope + unvalidated-const drifts + confirmed no false-confirm / no ship-gate leak across exhaustive probes. ### R-USSHORT-BATCH5-FORWARD-UNIVERSE-EXCHANGE-UNHASHABLE-BARE-TYPEERROR - the new offline forward_universe_snapshot builder's `_normalize_active_row` tests `exchange not in EXCHANGE_WHITELIST` (a frozenset) on the input row's unguarded `primary_exchange`/`exchange`, so an unhashable dict/list value raises a bare TypeError instead of the typed ForwardUniverseSnapshotError — a recurrence of the "unhashable value in {set} not inheriting the sibling isinstance guard" fail-closed class

- **Status**: **resolved** (Claude re-`审查` PASS 2026-07-06; committed this cycle). `_normalize_active_row` L216 now guards `if type(exchange) is not str or exchange not in EXCHANGE_WHITELIST` (short-circuit — a non-str value raises the typed error before the membership hash), so dict/list/bool `primary_exchange`/`exchange` fail closed as `ForwardUniverseSnapshotError`. Reviewer reproduced: `{}`/`[]`/`True` → typed `ForwardUniverseSnapshotError`, `"NYSE"` → accepted, `"OTHERX"` → typed. New public-path regression `test_rejects_non_string_exchange_without_typeerror` covers both ingress fields (`primary_exchange`, fallback `exchange`) × dict/list. Full offline `*us_short*` 3707 OK, zero regression. The identical sibling P3 fold-in `R-USSHORT-BATCH5-RUNFETCH-SCREEN-STATUS-UNHASHABLE-BARE-TYPEERROR` was NOT folded (Codex kept the repair runner-only) — it remains open for a later pick. (Was: open→in_progress P3; reviewer missed it in the integral read, independent §3.5 agent caught it, reviewer reproduced.)
- **Severity**: **P3 (independent agent: P2)** (whole-class fail-closed contract robustness, SAFE-direction + CONTAINED: `_normalize_active_row` L216 `if exchange not in EXCHANGE_WHITELIST` computes `hash(exchange)` before membership, so a dict/list `primary_exchange`/`exchange` raises bare `TypeError: unhashable type` instead of `ForwardUniverseSnapshotError`. Fails closed — the exception aborts inside the builder BEFORE `_write_json_atomic`, so no partial/tracked write, no leak, no fail-open; the input is a gitignored + real-`git check-ignore`-enforced locally-trusted `state/us_short` JSON; ungraceful exception TYPE only. The sibling `ticker` at L210 is safe because `canonical_us_ticker` isinstance-guards dict/list→None; the adjacent exchange membership did not inherit it).
- **Scope reviewed**: `runners/us_short_forward_universe_snapshot.py` `_normalize_active_row` L207-232 (esp. L215-216). Reviewer whole-class membership grep (L216 is the ONLY input-value set-membership test in the runner) + own reproduction + independent §3.5 main-tree read-only agent.
- **Technical finding**: L215 `exchange = row.get("primary_exchange", row.get("exchange"))` reads the input row value unguarded; L216 `if exchange not in EXCHANGE_WHITELIST:` (a frozenset) hashes it. Reviewer reproduced: `primary_exchange` `{"a":1}`/`["x"]` → bare `TypeError`; `"NYSE"` → accepted; `"OTHERX"` (invalid str) → typed `ForwardUniverseSnapshotError`. Reachable via `run_from_local_input`/CLI with a hand-crafted local input JSON row.
- **Verified correct (NOT findings — HELD)**: path confinement strong (input+output gitignored `state/us_short` via real `git check-ignore`, output canonical-pinned to `forward_universe_snapshot_<date>.json`, symlink/`..`/absolute/drive/URL/subdir all rejected); hash freeze integrity (validate recomputes both sha256 with sort_keys+separators, rejects post-hash mutation/mismatch/row_count/unsorted); forged safety claims rejected (schema `const false` + runner `is not False` identity, truthy-int rejected); fabrication rejected (duplicate/unsorted/non-active/non-whitelist/non-existent-or-non-gitignored source_ref); source_refs real-byte sha256'd; datetime class SAFE (strptime/fromisoformat in try/except ValueError, no astimezone overflow-op; boundary years/`+14:00`/huge strings → bool False, not bare); cik range-checked; schema↔runner consistent; offline no-network no-leak. Reviewer full offline `*us_short*` 3706 OK, doc/route 60 OK.
- **Required repair** (offline, this runner only): add `if type(exchange) is not str: raise ForwardUniverseSnapshotError(...)` (isinstance-str guard) BEFORE the L216 membership test, mirroring the ticker isinstance guard, so a non-str `primary_exchange`/`exchange` fails closed as `ForwardUniverseSnapshotError`. Add a regression test (dict/list exchange → `ForwardUniverseSnapshotError`, not bare `TypeError`). Do not change path/hash/schema logic or cross lanes. (Optional whole-class: fold the identical sibling `R-USSHORT-BATCH5-RUNFETCH-SCREEN-STATUS-UNHASHABLE-BARE-TYPEERROR` fix into the same repair.)

### R-USSHORT-BATCH5-DATA-CONTEXT-OBSERVED-AT-BARE-OVERFLOW-FAIL-CLOSED-CLASS - the new offline official-context assembler's `_observed_at_to_naive_et` converts a tz-aware observed_at via `astimezone().replace(tzinfo=None)`, so a boundary-year tz-aware timestamp raises a bare OverflowError instead of the typed DataContextAssemblyError — a recurrence of the datetime/numeric out-of-range fail-closed class, not inheriting the sibling reconciler `_parse_observed_at` guard

- **Status**: **resolved** (Claude re-`审查` PASS 2026-07-06; committed this cycle). `_observed_at_to_naive_et` now wraps the tz normalization in `try/except (OverflowError, ValueError, OSError) → DataContextAssemblyError`; reviewer reproduced both boundary-year tz-aware values (`0001-…+14:00`, `9999-…-14:00`) → typed `DataContextAssemblyError` (no bare `OverflowError`), and normal `2026-…` → `2026-06-29 08:00:00` (no over-reject); new public-path regression `test_resolved_pass2_sources_wrap_boundary_tz_observed_at_overflow` covers both boundary years. Full offline `*us_short*` 3687 OK, zero regression. Recurrence of the same whole-class fail-closed contract issue already resolved twice (`R-USSHORT-BATCH5-THEME-SOURCE-PACKET-TIMESTAMP-NUMERIC-FAIL-CLOSED-REGRESSION`, `R-USSHORT-BATCH5-MOMENTUM-PRICE-PACKET-NUMERIC-FAIL-CLOSED-CLASS`); flagged per the standing "recheck a new runner against the recently-fixed class" directive. (Was: open→in_progress P3.)
- **Severity**: **P3** (whole-class fail-closed contract robustness, SAFE-direction + CONTAINED: `_observed_at_to_naive_et` leaks a bare `OverflowError` only on a boundary-year (`0001`/`9999`) tz-aware `observed_at`, which local reviewed source artifacts do not realistically produce; the sole production caller `runners/us_short_batch5_data_context_source_packet.py` wraps assembly in `except Exception → SourcePacketError`, so the run fails CLOSED operationally with no bad output / no leak — only the PUBLIC assembler API `assemble_official_context_components_from_resolved_pass2_sources` leaks the untyped exception type. It is a contract-purity leg, not a live-provider hostile boundary like the two prior siblings).
- **Scope reviewed**: `runners/us_short_batch5_data_context.py` `_observed_at_to_naive_et` L157-166 (`dt.astimezone(ZoneInfo("America/New_York")).replace(tzinfo=None)`), reached via `_collect_observed_at_instants` and the `candidate_artifact.generated_at` path (~L805). Reviewer independent reproduction + independent §3.5 main-tree read-only agent (~52 probes).
- **Technical finding**: `_observed_at_to_naive_et` parses ISO then, for a tz-aware value, does `dt.astimezone(ZoneInfo("America/New_York")).replace(tzinfo=None)`. A boundary-year tz-aware value (`0001-01-01T00:00:00+14:00`, `9999-12-31T23:59:59-14:00`) under/overflows `datetime` during `astimezone` → bare `OverflowError: date value out of range`, not the typed `DataContextAssemblyError`. Reviewer reproduced both; a normal `2026-06-29T12:00:00+00:00` → `2026-06-29 08:00:00` (correct UTC→ET, no over-reject); the sibling reconciler `_parse_observed_at` sidesteps the whole class by REJECTING tz-aware values outright, a guard the new assembler did not inherit because it CONVERTS tz-aware instead.
- **Verified correct (NOT findings — HELD)**: the core no-dangling / provenance contract is sound — reconciler `_validate_source_refs` (L86-104) rejects empty/missing-key/duplicate/`://`/`\`/`:`/absolute/`/../` source_refs for ALL families with a hard index (no `.get()` fail-open); existence-binding is enforced producer-side via `_existing_repo_path`; the assembler binds each family's refs to the real consumed roles and cannot forge per-ticker identity (rejects pre-populated ticker/row_source/signals); schema↔reconciler are consistent (a path char-class drift is fail-closed in BOTH directions); the orchestrator consumes `run_provenance`/`per_ticker_analysis` under documented names and the orchestrator test was STRENGTHENED (new `test_source_refs_missing_input_family_rejected`), not weakened; no network import / no raw read / outputs gitignored / no secret leak. Reviewer full offline `*us_short*` 3686 OK, doc/route 60 OK, zero regression.
- **Required repair** (offline, this runner only): wrap the tz conversion (or the parse body) in `_observed_at_to_naive_et` so `OverflowError`/`ValueError` raise `DataContextAssemblyError` (mirror the whole-class typed-error contract every other bad input in this assembler already follows). Add a regression test: a boundary-year tz-aware `observed_at` reaching the assembler public path → `DataContextAssemblyError`, not a bare `OverflowError`. Do not change the source-ref binding / reconciler / schema logic or cross lanes.
- **Optional (non-blocking)**: (O2 P3) converge the reconciler `_validate_source_refs` path char-class check with the batch4 context-packet schema pattern — the reconciler rejects a bare `:` the schema pattern allows, and the schema rejects a `..` substring the reconciler's segment check allows; both are fail-closed, but a one-line convergence removes the latent drift.
- **Verification evidence**: reviewer reproduced bare `OverflowError` on both boundary-year tz-aware values + normal-value no-over-reject (`2026-...` → `08:00`) + sibling `_parse_observed_at` rejects tz-aware. Independent §3.5 agent reproduced the same and confirmed all six core invariants (no-dangling, reconciler completeness, schema/code consistency, consumption, offline/hygiene, same-class) HELD across ~52 probes; production caller wraps assembly → operationally fail-closed. Reviewer ran full offline `*us_short*` 3686 OK + doc/route 60 OK.
- **Closure**: Codex repair wraps timezone-normalization failures (`OverflowError`/`ValueError`/`OSError` → `DataContextAssemblyError`) and adds a public-assembler boundary-year regression for `0001-01-01T00:00:00+14:00` and `9999-12-31T23:59:59-14:00`; closure requires full offline green, Claude re-`审查 PASS`, then commit by Claude.

- **Severity**: **P2** (tracked-artifact false coverage attestation on the very block whose purpose is honest provider-health disclosure: no crash, no secret leak, and nothing downstream consumes this block programmatically [`overall_run_state=usable_with_fallback` is still correct via worst-of], but a COMMITTED provenance file states 230 unresolved SEC-missing-market-cap names when the true figure is 565 — a ~2.46x optimistic understatement that directly contradicts `pass1_result.needs_market_cap=565` in the SAME file, and no guard catches it. - **Scope reviewed**: uncommitted `docs/us_short_universe_fetch_summary_20260706.json` `provider_health.opportunistic_fallbacks.fmp_profile_market_cap`; `runners/us_short_universe_fetch.py` `_build_run_fetch_provider_health` L156-208 + call site L1229-1234 + `fallback_targets`/`fmp_attempted` L1169-1172. - **Technical finding**: `fallback_targets = summary_counts["needs_market_cap"]` (pre-rescue, L1169); `fmp_attempted = min(len(fallback_targets), 240)` (L1172); the builder receives `fallback_needed_count=len(fallback_targets)` (L1231) and sets `needed_count=fallback_needed_count`, `unresolved_count=max(needed-rescued,0)`. A genuine block for this run = needed 575 / attempted 240 / rescued 10 / unresolved 565 (unresolved == the final needs_market_cap). The tracked block instead says needed 240 / unresolved 230 — `needed_count` was set to the capped `attempted` (240), giving unresolved 240-10=230. `attempted=240`, `rescued=10`, `overall_run_state=usable_with_fallback` are correct.
